<script>
"use strict";

const VIEWS = [
  "system",
  "architecture",
  "kindred",
  "canon",
  "ai",
  "runtime",
  "why"
];

const state = {
  data: null,
  search: [],
  current: "system",
  tour: false,
  tourTimer: null,
  graph: null
};

const $ = selector =>
  document.querySelector(selector);

const $$ = selector =>
  [...document.querySelectorAll(selector)];

const esc = value =>
  String(value ?? "")
    .replaceAll("&","&amp;")
    .replaceAll("<","&lt;")
    .replaceAll(">","&gt;")
    .replaceAll('"',"&quot;");

const fmt = value =>
  Number(value || 0)
    .toLocaleString();

function titleCase(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .replaceAll("_"," ")
    .replaceAll("-"," ")
    .replace(
      /\b\w/g,
      letter => letter.toUpperCase()
    );
}

function metric(
  label,
  value,
  bar = 50
) {
  return `
    <div
      class="card metric"
      style="--bar:${bar}%"
    >
      <div
        class="metric-value"
        data-count="${Number(value || 0)}"
      >
        0
      </div>

      <div class="metric-label">
        ${esc(label)}
      </div>

      <div class="metric-bar"></div>
    </div>
  `;
}

function animateCounters(root = document) {
  root
    .querySelectorAll("[data-count]")
    .forEach(element => {

      const target =
        Number(element.dataset.count || 0);

      const start =
        performance.now();

      const duration =
        Math.min(
          1300,
          500 +
          Math.log10(target + 1) * 170
        );

      function frame(now) {

        const t =
          Math.min(
            1,
            (now - start) / duration
          );

        const eased =
          1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 4);

        element.textContent =
          Math.floor(
            target * eased
          ).toLocaleString();

        if (t < 1) {
          requestAnimationFrame(frame);
        }
      }

      requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    });
}

function navigate(name) {

  if (!VIEWS.includes(name)) {
    return;
  }

  state.current = name;

  $$(".view").forEach(view => {
    view.classList.toggle(
      "active",
      view.dataset.view === name
    );
  });

  $$(".nav-btn").forEach(button => {
    button.classList.toggle(
      "active",
      button.dataset.target === name
    );
  });

  const index =
    VIEWS.indexOf(name);

  $("#progress").style.width =
    `${((index + 1) / VIEWS.length) * 100}%`;

  history.replaceState(
    null,
    "",
    "#" + name
  );

  const active =
    document.querySelector(
      `.view[data-view="${name}"]`
    );

  if (active) {
    active.scrollTop = 0;
    animateCounters(active);
  }
}

function openDrawer(
  type,
  title,
  value
) {

  $("#drawerType").textContent =
    type;

  $("#drawerTitle").textContent =
    title;

  $("#drawerMeta").textContent =
    "Verified snapshot projection";

  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML =
    `<pre>${esc(
      JSON.stringify(
        value,
        null,
        2
      )
    )}</pre>`;

  $("#drawer")
    .classList.add("open");
}

function closeDrawer() {
  $("#drawer")
    .classList.remove("open");
}

function renderMetrics(data) {

  const m =
    data.metrics || {};

  const max =
    Math.max(
      ...Object.values(m)
        .filter(Number.isFinite),
      1
    );

  const bar = value =>
    Math.max(
      7,
      Math.min(
        100,
        Math.log10(
          Number(value || 0) + 1
        ) /
        Math.log10(max + 1) *
        100
      )
    );

  $("#metrics").innerHTML = [
    metric(
      "Files",
      m.files,
      bar(m.files)
    ),
    metric(
      "Relationships",
      m.constitutional_relationships,
      bar(m.constitutional_relationships)
    ),
    metric(
      "Disciplines",
      m.kindred_disciplines,
      bar(m.kindred_disciplines)
    ),
    metric(
      "Tests discovered",
      m.test_functions,
      bar(m.test_functions)
    )
  ].join("");

  $("#runtimeMetrics").innerHTML = [
    metric(
      "Files",
      m.files,
      bar(m.files)
    ),
    metric(
      "Directories",
      m.directories,
      bar(m.directories)
    ),
    metric(
      "Authority records",
      m.authority_records,
      bar(m.authority_records)
    ),
    metric(
      "Exiles",
      m.exiles,
      bar(m.exiles)
    ),
    metric(
      "Relationships",
      m.constitutional_relationships,
      bar(m.constitutional_relationships)
    ),
    metric(
      "Disciplines",
      m.kindred_disciplines,
      bar(m.kindred_disciplines)
    ),
    metric(
      "Test files",
      m.test_files,
      bar(m.test_files)
    ),
    metric(
      "Test functions",
      m.test_functions,
      bar(m.test_functions)
    )
  ].join("");

  animateCounters();
}

function renderAuthority(data) {

  const statuses =
    data.authority_statuses || {};

  const total =
    Object.values(statuses)
      .reduce(
        (a,b) => a + Number(b || 0),
        0
      );

  $("#authoritySummary").textContent =
    `${fmt(total)} traced authority records`;

  $("#authorityPills").innerHTML =
    Object.entries(statuses)
      .map(([key,value]) => `
        <span class="pill">
          ${esc(key)} · ${fmt(value)}
        </span>
      `)
      .join("");
}

function renderedifice(data) {

  $("#edifice").innerHTML =
    (data.identity_edifice || [])
      .map((name,index) => `
        <div class="tier">

          <div class="tier-box">
            <div class="tier-name">
              ${esc(name)}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="tier-number">
            ${String(index + 1).padStart(2,"0")}
          </div>

        </div>
      `)
      .join("");
}

function createItem(
  index,
  title,
  copy,
  value,
  type,
  badge = ""
) {

  const element =
    document.createElement("article");

  element.className =
    "card item";

  element.innerHTML = `
    <div class="item-index">
      ${String(index + 1).padStart(2,"0")}
    </div>

    <h3>
      ${esc(title)}
    </h3>

    <p>
      ${esc(copy || "")}
    </p>

    ${
      badge
        ? `
          <span class="badge ${esc(badge)}">
            ${esc(badge)}
          </span>
        `
        : ""
    }
  `;

  element.addEventListener(
    "click",
    () => openDrawer(
      type,
      title,
      value
    )
  );

  return element;
}

function renderSubstrates(data) {

  const root =
    $("#substrates");

  root.innerHTML = "";

  (data.living_substrates || [])
    .forEach((item,index) => {

      const title =
        item.id ||
        item.name ||
        `Substrate ${index + 1}`;

      root.appendChild(
        createItem(
          index,
          titleCase(title),
          item.purpose || "",
          item,
          "Living Substrate"
        )
      );
    });
}

function renderDisciplines(data) {

  const root =
    $("#disciplines");

  root.innerHTML = "";

  (data.kindred_disciplines || [])
    .forEach((item,index) => {

      const title =
        item.canonical ||
        item.name ||
        item.id ||
        `Discipline ${index + 1}`;

      const relationCount =
        Array.isArray(item.relations)
          ? item.relations.length
          : 0;

      const copy =
        [
          relationCount
            ? `${relationCount} relationship forms`
            : "Relational discipline",
          item.steward || ""
        ]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(" · ");

      root.appendChild(
        createItem(
          index,
          titleCase(title),
          copy,
          item,
          "Kindred Discipline"
        )
      );
    });
}

function renderAuthorities(data) {

  const root =
    $("#authorities");

  root.innerHTML = "";

  (data.authority_records || [])
    .forEach((item,index) => {

      const title =
        item.title ||
        item.id ||
        item.path ||
        `Authority ${index + 1}`;

      const element =
        createItem(
          index,
          title,
          item.path || item.kind || "",
          item,
          "Authority Record",
          item.status || ""
        );

      root.appendChild(element);
    });
}

function renderAI(data) {

  const ai =
    data.ai || {};

  const mode =
    String(
      ai.mode || "native"
    ).toUpperCase();

  $("#aiMode").textContent =
    mode;

  $("#aiDescription").textContent =
    mode === "NATIVE"
      ? (
        "Savant is operating without external AI credentials. " +
        "Its native architecture remains valid and usable. " +
        "Configured providers can add capabilities without " +
        "changing the identity of the elements that consume them."
      )
      : (
        "External AI capability is active while Savant retains " +
        "native fallback. Provider activation augments execution " +
        "rather than becoming a new source of structural identity."
      );

  const providers =
    ai.providers || {};

  const root =
    $("#providers");

  if (!Object.keys(providers).length) {

    root.innerHTML = `
      <div class="provider">

        <div class="provider-dot"></div>

        <div class="provider-name">
          Provider registry
        </div>

        <div class="provider-state">
          native
        </div>

      </div>
    `;

    return;
  }

  root.innerHTML =
    Object.entries(providers)
      .map(([name,provider]) => `
        <div class="
          provider
          ${provider.usable ? "usable" : ""}
        ">

          <div class="provider-dot"></div>

          <div class="provider-name">
            ${esc(name)}
          </div>

          <div class="provider-state">
            ${
              provider.usable
                ? "active"
                : esc(
                    provider.reason ||
                    "inactive"
                  )
            }
          </div>

        </div>
      `)
      .join("");
}

function renderExiles(data) {

  const root =
    $("#exiles");

  root.innerHTML = "";

  (data.exiles || [])
    .forEach((item,index) => {

      const title =
        item.title ||
        item.name ||
        item.id ||
        `Exile ${index + 1}`;

      const purpose =
        Array.isArray(item.purpose)
          ? item.purpose.join(" · ")
          : item.purpose || "";

      root.appendChild(
        createItem(
          index,
          title,
          purpose,
          item,
          "Exile",
          item.status || ""
        )
      );
    });
}

function renderWhy(data) {

  const why =
    data.presentation?.why || [
      "To preserve meaning instead of merely storing output.",
      "To make complex systems composable instead of disposable.",
      "To keep authority, provenance and history visible.",
      "To let intelligence augment structure without owning it.",
      "To build something capable of becoming more than a collection of scripts."
    ];

  $("#whyList").innerHTML =
    why.map(line => `
      <div class="why-line">
        ${esc(line)}
      </div>
    `).join("");
}

function buildSearch(data) {

  const items = [];

  function add(
    type,
    title,
    value
  ) {

    if (!title) {
      return;
    }

    items.push({
      type,
      title: String(title),
      value
    });
  }

  (data.exiles || [])
    .forEach(item =>
      add(
        "Exile",
        item.title ||
        item.name ||
        item.id,
        item
      )
    );

  (data.authority_records || [])
    .forEach(item =>
      add(
        "Authority",
        item.title ||
        item.id ||
        item.path,
        item
      )
    );

  (data.kindred_disciplines || [])
    .forEach(item =>
      add(
        "Discipline",
        item.canonical ||
        item.name ||
        item.id,
        item
      )
    );

  (data.constitutional_relationships || [])
    .forEach(item =>
      add(
        "Relationship",
        item.name ||
        item.id ||
        item.canonical,
        item
      )
    );

  (data.living_substrates || [])
    .forEach(item =>
      add(
        "Substrate",
        item.id ||
        item.name,
        item
      )
    );

  state.search = items;
}

function showSearch() {

  $("#command")
    .classList.add("open");

  $("#commandInput").value =
    "";

  renderSearch("");

  setTimeout(
    () =>
      $("#commandInput").focus(),
    20
  );
}

function hideSearch() {
  $("#command")
    .classList.remove("open");
}

function renderSearch(query) {

  const q =
    query
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();

  const results =
    state.search
      .filter(item => {

        if (!q) {
          return true;
        }

        const corpus =
          `${item.title} ${item.type} ${
            JSON.stringify(item.value)
          }`
            .toLowerCase();

        return corpus.includes(q);
      })
      .slice(0,32);

  const root =
    $("#commandResults");

  root.innerHTML = "";

  results.forEach(item => {

    const row =
      document.createElement("div");

    row.className =
      "result";

    row.innerHTML = `
      <strong>
        ${esc(item.title)}
      </strong>

      <span>
        ${esc(item.type)}
      </span>
    `;

    row.addEventListener(
      "click",
      () => {
        hideSearch();

        openDrawer(
          item.type,
          item.title,
          item.value
        );
      }
    );

    root.appendChild(row);
  });
}

/* BACKGROUND FIELD */

function createSpace() {

  const canvas =
    $("#space");

  const ctx =
    canvas.getContext("2d");

  let width = 0;
  let height = 0;
  let stars = [];

  function resize() {

    const dpr =
      Math.min(
        window.devicePixelRatio || 1,
        2
      );

    width = innerWidth;
    height = innerHeight;

    canvas.width =
      width * dpr;

    canvas.height =
      height * dpr;

    canvas.style.width =
      width + "px";

    canvas.style.height =
      height + "px";

    ctx.setTransform(
      dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0
    );

    const count =
      Math.min(
        210,
        Math.max(
          70,
          Math.floor(
            width * height / 6500
          )
        )
      );

    stars =
      Array.from(
        {length: count},
        () => ({
          x: Math.random() * width,
          y: Math.random() * height,
          r: Math.random() * 1.15 + .15,
          a: Math.random() * .48 + .05,
          d: Math.random() * .15 + .02
        })
      );
  }

  let pointerX = 0;
  let pointerY = 0;

  addEventListener(
    "pointermove",
    event => {

      pointerX =
        event.clientX - width / 2;

      pointerY =
        event.clientY - height / 2;

      document.documentElement
        .style.setProperty(
          "--mx",
          `${event.clientX}px`
        );

      document.documentElement
        .style.setProperty(
          "--my",
          `${event.clientY}px`
        );
    },
    {passive: true}
  );

  function draw() {

    ctx.clearRect(
      0,0,width,height
    );

    for (const star of stars) {

      const x =
        star.x +
        pointerX * star.d * .025;

      const y =
        star.y +
        pointerY * star.d * .025;

      ctx.beginPath();

      ctx.arc(
        x,
        y,
        star.r,
        0,
        Math.PI * 2
      );

      ctx.fillStyle =
        `rgba(255,255,255,${star.a})`;

      ctx.fill();
    }

    requestAnimationFrame(draw);
  }

  resize();

  addEventListener(
    "resize",
    resize
  );

  draw();
}

/* INTERACTIVE TOPOLOGY */

function createGraph(data) {

  const canvas =
    $("#graph");

  const ctx =
    canvas.getContext("2d");

  let width = 0;
  let height = 0;
  let dpr = 1;

  const source =
    (data.exiles || [])
      .slice(0,18);

  const nodes =
    source.map(
      (item,index) => ({
        item,
        index,
        angle:
          Math.PI * 2 *
          index /
          Math.max(source.length,1),
        radius:
          .33 +
          (index % 3) * .055,
        x: 0,
        y: 0,
        vx: 0,
        vy: 0,
        hover: false
      })
    );

  let pointer = {
    x: -9999,
    y: -9999,
    down: false
  };

  function resize() {

    const rect =
      canvas.getBoundingClientRect();

    width = rect.width;
    height = rect.height;

    dpr =
      Math.min(
        window.devicePixelRatio || 1,
        2
      );

    canvas.width =
      width * dpr;

    canvas.height =
      height * dpr;

    ctx.setTransform(
      dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0
    );
  }

  function target(node,time) {

    const min =
      Math.min(width,height);

    const wobble =
      Math.sin(
        time * .00022 +
        node.index * 1.7
      ) * 7;

    return {
      x:
        width / 2 +
        Math.cos(
          node.angle +
          time * .000025 *
          (node.index % 2 ? 1 : -1)
        ) *
        min *
        node.radius +
        wobble,

      y:
        height / 2 +
        Math.sin(
          node.angle +
          time * .000025 *
          (node.index % 2 ? 1 : -1)
        ) *
        min *
        node.radius *
        .78
    };
  }

  function label(node) {

    return (
      node.item.title ||
      node.item.name ||
      node.item.id ||
      `Exile ${node.index + 1}`
    );
  }

  function draw(time) {

    ctx.clearRect(
      0,0,width,height
    );

    const cx =
      width / 2;

    const cy =
      height / 2;

    const min =
      Math.min(width,height);

    /* orbital fields */

    for (
      const factor of
      [.18,.31,.43]
    ) {

      ctx.beginPath();

      ctx.ellipse(
        cx,
        cy,
        min * factor,
        min * factor * .78,
        0,
        0,
        Math.PI * 2
      );

      ctx.strokeStyle =
        "rgba(255,255,255,.055)";

      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.stroke();
    }

    /* center */

    ctx.save();

    ctx.translate(cx,cy);

    ctx.rotate(
      Math.PI / 4 +
      time * .000035
    );

    ctx.strokeStyle =
      "rgba(217,181,109,.78)";

    ctx.lineWidth = 1;

    ctx.strokeRect(
      -44,
      -44,
      88,
      88
    );

    ctx.strokeStyle =
      "rgba(217,181,109,.25)";

    ctx.strokeRect(
      -27,
      -27,
      54,
      54
    );

    ctx.restore();

    /* update */

    nodes.forEach(node => {

      const t =
        target(node,time);

      node.vx +=
        (t.x - node.x) * .003;

      node.vy +=
        (t.y - node.y) * .003;

      node.vx *= .93;
      node.vy *= .93;

      node.x += node.vx;
      node.y += node.vy;

      if (
        !Number.isFinite(node.x) ||
        node.x === 0
      ) {
        node.x = t.x;
        node.y = t.y;
      }

      const dx =
        pointer.x - node.x;

      const dy =
        pointer.y - node.y;

      node.hover =
        Math.hypot(dx,dy) < 31;
    });

    /* graph edges */

    nodes.forEach((node,index) => {

      const next =
        nodes[
          (index + 1) %
          nodes.length
        ];

      if (!next) {
        return;
      }

      ctx.beginPath();

      ctx.moveTo(
        node.x,
        node.y
      );

      ctx.lineTo(
        next.x,
        next.y
      );

      ctx.strokeStyle =
        "rgba(217,181,109,.075)";

      ctx.stroke();

      ctx.beginPath();

      ctx.moveTo(
        cx,
        cy
      );

      ctx.lineTo(
        node.x,
        node.y
      );

      ctx.strokeStyle =
        node.hover
          ? "rgba(217,181,109,.34)"
          : "rgba(255,255,255,.045)";

      ctx.stroke();
    });

    /* nodes */

    nodes.forEach(node => {

      const active =
        node.hover;

      const r =
        active ? 25 : 21;

      ctx.beginPath();

      ctx.arc(
        node.x,
        node.y,
        r,
        0,
        Math.PI * 2
      );

      ctx.fillStyle =
        active
          ? "rgba(217,181,109,.12)"
          : "rgba(7,9,13,.88)";

      ctx.fill();

      ctx.strokeStyle =
        active
          ? "rgba(217,181,109,.85)"
          : "rgba(255,255,255,.16)";

      ctx.stroke();

      ctx.fillStyle =
        active
          ? "#f0d59b"
          : "#c5c7cc";

      ctx.font =
        "8px Inter, sans-serif";

      ctx.textAlign =
        "center";

      ctx.textBaseline =
        "middle";

      let text =
        label(node)
          .toUpperCase();

      if (text.length > 12) {
        text =
          text.slice(0,11) + "…";
      }

      ctx.fillText(
        text,
        node.x,
        node.y
      );
    });

    requestAnimationFrame(draw);
  }

  function point(event) {

    const rect =
      canvas.getBoundingClientRect();

    pointer.x =
      event.clientX - rect.left;

    pointer.y =
      event.clientY - rect.top;
  }

  canvas.addEventListener(
    "pointermove",
    event => {

      point(event);

      if (pointer.down) {

        nodes.forEach(node => {

          const dx =
            node.x - pointer.x;

          const dy =
            node.y - pointer.y;

          const distance =
            Math.max(
              30,
              Math.hypot(dx,dy)
            );

          if (distance < 120) {

            node.vx +=
              dx / distance * 1.8;

            node.vy +=
              dy / distance * 1.8;
          }
        });
      }

      canvas.style.cursor =
        nodes.some(
          node => node.hover
        )
          ? "pointer"
          : "crosshair";
    }
  );

  canvas.addEventListener(
    "pointerdown",
    event => {
      pointer.down = true;
      point(event);
    }
  );

  addEventListener(
    "pointerup",
    () => {
      pointer.down = false;
    }
  );

  canvas.addEventListener(
    "click",
    event => {

      point(event);

      const node =
        nodes.find(
          item =>
            Math.hypot(
              pointer.x - item.x,
              pointer.y - item.y
            ) < 32
        );

      if (node) {

        openDrawer(
          "Exile",
          label(node),
          node.item
        );
      }
    }
  );

  resize();

  addEventListener(
    "resize",
    resize
  );

  requestAnimationFrame(draw);

  state.graph = {
    nodes
  };
}

/* CARD LIGHTING */

function enableCardLighting() {

  document.addEventListener(
    "pointermove",
    event => {

      const card =
        event.target.closest(".card");

      if (!card) {
        return;
      }

      const rect =
        card.getBoundingClientRect();

      card.style.setProperty(
        "--card-x",
        `${event.clientX - rect.left}px`
      );

      card.style.setProperty(
        "--card-y",
        `${event.clientY - rect.top}px`
      );
    },
    {passive: true}
  );
}

  },

  },

    title: "Measured implementation",
    copy:
      "The numbers shown here are generated from the runtime snapshot rather than typed into the presentation."

    view: "why",
    title: "Why it exists",
    copy:
      "Savant is an attempt to make complex creative and computational systems retain meaning, history and extensibility."
  }
];

function stopTour() {

  state.tour = false;

  clearTimeout(
    state.tourTimer
  );

  $("#tourBtn").textContent =
    "Tour";

  $("#tourToast")
    .classList.remove("show");
}

function startTour() {

  if (state.tour) {
    stopTour();
    return;
  }

  state.tour = true;

  $("#tourBtn").textContent =
    "Stop";

  let index = 0;

  function step() {

    if (!state.tour) {
      return;
    }

    const item =
      TOUR[index];

    navigate(item.view);

    $("#tourTitle").textContent =
      item.title;

    $("#tourCopy").textContent =
      item.copy;

    $("#tourToast")
      .classList.add("show");

    index++;

    if (index >= TOUR.length) {

      state.tourTimer =
        setTimeout(
          stopTour,
          7000
        );

      return;
    }

    state.tourTimer =
      setTimeout(
        step,
        7000
      );
  }

  step();
}

/* FULLSCREEN */

async function toggleFullscreen() {

  try {

    if (!document.fullscreenElement) {

      await document.documentElement
        .requestFullscreen();

      $("#fullBtn").textContent =
        "Exit";
    } else {

      await document
        .exitFullscreen();

      $("#fullBtn").textContent =
        "Fullscreen";
    }

  } catch (_) {
    /* browser may deny fullscreen */
  }
}

/* RENDER */

function render(data) {

  state.data =
    data;

  const metrics =
    data.metrics || {};

  $("#runtimeState").textContent =
    `Verified snapshot · ${
      fmt(metrics.files)
    } files`;

  $("#snapshotHash").textContent =
    "SHA · " +
    String(
      data.snapshot_sha256 || ""
    ).slice(0,16);

  renderMetrics(data);
  renderAuthority(data);
  renderedifice(data);
  renderSubstrates(data);
  renderDisciplines(data);
  renderAuthorities(data);
  renderAI(data);
  renderExiles(data);
  renderWhy(data);
  buildSearch(data);
  createGraph(data);

  const requested =
    location.hash
      .replace("#","");

  if (VIEWS.includes(requested)) {
    navigate(requested);
  } else {
    navigate("system");
  }
}

/* EVENTS */

$$(".nav-btn")
  .forEach(button => {

    button.addEventListener(
      "click",
      () => {
        stopTour();

        navigate(
          button.dataset.target
        );
      }
    );
  });

$("#enter")
  .addEventListener(
    "click",
    () => {

      $("#boot")
        .classList.add("gone");

      setTimeout(
        () => animateCounters(),
        350
      );
    }
  );

$("#drawerClose")
  .addEventListener(
    "click",
    closeDrawer
  );

$("#searchBtn")
  .addEventListener(
    "click",
    showSearch
  );

$("#tourBtn")
  .addEventListener(
    "click",
    startTour
  );

$("#fullBtn")
  .addEventListener(
    "click",
    toggleFullscreen
  );

$("#command")
  .addEventListener(
    "click",
    event => {

      if (
        event.target ===
        $("#command")
      ) {
        hideSearch();
      }
    }
  );

$("#commandInput")
  .addEventListener(
    "input",
    event =>
      renderSearch(
        event.target.value
      )
  );

document.addEventListener(
  "keydown",
  event => {

    const typing =
      ["INPUT","TEXTAREA"]
        .includes(
          document.activeElement.tagName
        );

    if (
      event.key === "/" &&
      !typing
    ) {

      event.preventDefault();
      showSearch();
      return;
    }

    if (
      (event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) &&
      event.key.toLowerCase() === "k"
    ) {

      event.preventDefault();
      showSearch();
      return;
    }

    if (event.key === "Escape") {

      hideSearch();
      closeDrawer();
      stopTour();

      return;
    }

    if (
      !typing &&
      event.key >= "1" &&
      event.key <= "7"
    ) {

      navigate(
        VIEWS[
          Number(event.key) - 1
        ]
      );
    }

    if (
      !typing &&
      event.key === "ArrowRight"
    ) {

      const index =
        VIEWS.indexOf(
          state.current
        );

      navigate(
        VIEWS[
          Math.min(
            VIEWS.length - 1,
            index + 1
          )
        ]
      );
    }

    if (
      !typing &&
      event.key === "ArrowLeft"
    ) {

      const index =
        VIEWS.indexOf(
          state.current
        );

      navigate(
        VIEWS[
          Math.max(
            0,
            index - 1
          )
        ]
      );
    }
  }
);

/* INIT */

async function init() {

  createSpace();
  enableCardLighting();

  try {

    const response =
      await fetch(
        "../data/savant.snapshot.json",
        {
          cache: "no-store"
        }
      );

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(
        `HTTP ${response.status}`
      );
    }

    const data =
      await response.json();

    render(data);

  } catch (error) {

    console.error(error);

    $("#error")
      .classList.add("show");
  }
}

init();
</script>

(function(){
  const navButtons=[...document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn')];
  navButtons.forEach((b,i)=>b.dataset.index=String(i+1).padStart(2,'0'));
  const orbit=document.getElementById('cursorOrbit');
  document.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(orbit){orbit.style.left=e.clientX+'px';orbit.style.top=e.clientY+'px'};document.body.classList.toggle('pointer-hot',!!e.target.closest('button,.card,.result,[data-inspect]'))},{passive:true});
  let toastTimer; window.savantToast=function(msg){const t=document.getElementById('toast');if(!t)return;t.textContent=msg;t.classList.add('show');clearTimeout(toastTimer);toastTimer=setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('show'),1700)};
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(!document.hidden && state.data) savantToast('Observatory synchronized')});
  window.addEventListener('online',()=>savantToast('Network restored'));
  window.addEventListener('offline',()=>savantToast('Offline — current snapshot retained'));
  document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='?'&&!['INPUT','TEXTAREA'].includes(document.activeElement.tagName)){e.preventDefault();savantToast('Keys: 1–7 views · ← → navigate · / search · Esc close')}});
  let touchX=null; document.querySelector('.stage').addEventListener('touchstart',e=>{touchX=e.changedTouches[0].clientX},{passive:true});
  document.querySelector('.stage').addEventListener('touchend',e=>{if(touchX===null)return;const dx=e.changedTouches[0].clientX-touchX;touchX=null;if(Math.abs(dx)<70)return;const i=VIEWS.indexOf(state.current);navigate(VIEWS[Math.max(0,Math.min(VIEWS.length-1,i+(dx<0?1:-1)))])},{passive:true});
  const observer=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>entries.forEach(x=>{if(x.isIntersecting)x.target.classList.add('observed')}),{threshold:.12});
  document.querySelectorAll('.card').forEach(x=>observer.observe(x));
  document.addEventListener('dblclick',e=>{const card=e.target.closest('.card');if(card){card.animate([{transform:'scale(1)'},{transform:'scale(1.012)'},{transform:'scale(1)'}],{duration:320,easing:'cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1)'})}});
  document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange',()=>{const b=document.getElementById('fullBtn');if(b)b.textContent=document.fullscreenElement?'Exit':'Fullscreen'});
})();

<script>
/* FOUNDER'S DEMONSTRATION CONTROLLER v3 */
(function(){
  const $d=s=>document.querySelector(s);
  const hud=$d("#demoHud");
  if(!hud) return;

  let index=0,timer=null,paused=false;
  const duration=9000;

  function data(){return (window.state&&window.state.data)||null}
  function metric(key,fallback="—"){
    const d=data(); return d&&d.metrics&&d.metrics[key]!=null ? Number(d.metrics[key]).toLocaleString() : fallback;
  }
  function p(){const d=data();return d&&d.presentation?d.presentation:{}}
  function why(i,fallback){const a=p().why||[];return a[i]||fallback}

  const slides=[
    ()=>({view:"system",k:"01 · Evidence",t:"This is not a mock statistic.",c:`The Observatory is reading a verified runtime projection: ${metric("files")} files across ${metric("directories")} directories. The interface is disposable; the runtime remains the source.`,m:[`${metric("authority_records")} authority records`,`${metric("test_functions")} test functions`]}),
    ()=>({view:"architecture",k:"02 · Architecture",t:"Complexity without duplication.",c:"Savant separates recursive identity from reusable implementation substrate. Higher structures emerge by composition, attachment, specialization and projection rather than copying lower-level substance.",m:["9 identity tiers","9 living substrates"]}),
    ()=>({view:"kindred",k:"03 · Relationships",t:"Relationship becomes infrastructure.",c:"Kindred makes dependency, lineage, provenance, evidence, compatibility, composition and other relational disciplines graph-addressable and reusable instead of scattering them through ad-hoc code.",m:[`${metric("kindred_disciplines")} disciplines`,`${metric("constitutional_relationships")} constitutional relations`]}),
    ()=>({view:"canon",k:"04 · Memory",t:"The system remembers how it became true.",c:why(1,"Savant preserves history as it evolves.")+" "+why(2,"Authority, lineage and provenance remain visible."),m:["canonical ≠ accepted ≠ provisional","supersession preserved"]}),
    ()=>({view:"ai",k:"05 · Intelligence",t:"AI is capability, not authority.",c:why(3,"Savant remains useful without an external AI provider and can become more capable when providers are available."),m:[`mode: ${(data()?.ai?.mode||"native")}`,"provider state is explicit"]}),
    ()=>({view:"runtime",k:"06 · Specialization",t:"Many owners. One composable system.",c:`The runtime currently projects ${metric("exiles")} Exiles. Each owns a bounded semantic responsibility while sharing reusable constitutional and relational machinery.`,m:["ownership remains explicit","extensions do not require replacement"]}),
    ()=>({view:"why",k:"07 · Purpose",t:"Why build Savant?",c:why(0,"To build complex intelligent software while preserving where knowledge came from.")+" "+why(4,"To make an ambitious system composable instead of brittle."),m:["lineage","provenance","replay","extensibility"]}),
    ()=>({view:"why",k:"08 · Dedication",t:p().dedication||"For Mom.",c:"This demonstration is a projection of the work itself: a system designed to grow without erasing its history, and to make the structure behind that growth visible.",m:[p().edition||"Founder's Demonstration",p().date||"August 2026"],final:true})
  ];

  function goView(v){
    if(typeof window.navigate==="function") window.navigate(v);
    else{
      document.querySelectorAll(".view").forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle("active",x.dataset.view===v));
      document.querySelectorAll(".nav-btn").forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle("active",x.dataset.target===v));
    }
  }
  function render(){
    const s=slides[index]();
    goView(s.view);
    $d("#demoKicker").textContent=s.k;
    $d("#demoTitle").textContent=s.t;
    $d("#demoTitle").classList.toggle("demo-final",!!s.final);
    $d("#demoCopy").textContent=s.c;
    $d("#demoMeta").innerHTML=s.m.map(x=>`<span>${String(x)}</span>`).join("<span>·</span>");
    $d("#demoProgress").style.width=((index+1)/slides.length*100)+"%";
    $d("#demoPrev").disabled=index===0;
    $d("#demoNext").textContent=index===slides.length-1?"Finish":"Next";
    schedule();
  }
  function schedule(){
    clearTimeout(timer);
    if(paused) return;
    timer=setTimeout(()=>{if(index<slides.length-1){index++;render()}else stop()},duration);
  }
  function start(){
    if(!data()) return;
    if(typeof window.stopTour==="function") window.stopTour();
    index=0;paused=false;
    document.body.classList.add("demo-mode");
    hud.classList.add("show");
    $d("#demoPause").textContent="Pause";
    render();
  }
  function stop(){
    clearTimeout(timer);
    hud.classList.remove("show");
    document.body.classList.remove("demo-mode");
    paused=false;
  }
  function next(){if(index<slides.length-1){index++;render()}else stop()}
  function prev(){if(index>0){index--;render()}}
  function pause(){paused=!paused;$d("#demoPause").textContent=paused?"Resume":"Pause";schedule()}

  const tour=$d("#tourBtn");
  if(tour){
    const clone=tour.cloneNode(true);
    tour.parentNode.replaceChild(clone,tour);
    clone.textContent="Demonstrate";
    clone.addEventListener("click",start);
  }
  $d("#demoNext").addEventListener("click",next);
  $d("#demoPrev").addEventListener("click",prev);
  $d("#demoPause").addEventListener("click",pause);
  $d("#demoExit").addEventListener("click",stop);
  document.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{
    if(!hud.classList.contains("show")) return;
    if(e.key==="Escape"){e.preventDefault();stop()}
    else if(e.key==="ArrowRight"){e.preventDefault();next()}
    else if(e.key==="ArrowLeft"){e.preventDefault();prev()}
    else if(e.key===" "){e.preventDefault();pause()}
  });
  window.startDemonstration=start;
})();
</script>

</body>
</html>

<script>
"use strict";

const VIEWS = [
  "system",
  "spatial",
  "architecture",
  "kindred",
  "canon",
  "ai",
  "runtime",
  "why"
];

const state = {
  data: null,
  search: [],
  current: "system",
  tour: false,
  tourTimer: null,
  graph: null
};

const $ = selector =>
  document.querySelector(selector);

const $$ = selector =>
  [...document.querySelectorAll(selector)];

const esc = value =>
  String(value ?? "")
    .replaceAll("&","&amp;")
    .replaceAll("<","&lt;")
    .replaceAll(">","&gt;")
    .replaceAll('"',"&quot;");

const fmt = value =>
  Number(value || 0)
    .toLocaleString();

function titleCase(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .replaceAll("_"," ")
    .replaceAll("-"," ")
    .replace(
      /\b\w/g,
      letter => letter.toUpperCase()
    );
}

function metric(
  label,
  value,
  bar = 50
) {
  return `
    <div
      class="card metric"
      style="--bar:${bar}%"
    >
      <div
        class="metric-value"
        data-count="${Number(value || 0)}"
      >
        0
      </div>

      <div class="metric-label">
        ${esc(label)}
      </div>

      <div class="metric-bar"></div>
    </div>
  `;
}

function animateCounters(root = document) {
  root
    .querySelectorAll("[data-count]")
    .forEach(element => {

      const target =
        Number(element.dataset.count || 0);

      const start =
        performance.now();

      const duration =
        Math.min(
          1300,
          500 +
          Math.log10(target + 1) * 170
        );

      function frame(now) {

        const t =
          Math.min(
            1,
            (now - start) / duration
          );

        const eased =
          1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 4);

        element.textContent =
          Math.floor(
            target * eased
          ).toLocaleString();

        if (t < 1) {
          requestAnimationFrame(frame);
        }
      }

      requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    });
}

function navigate(name) {

  if (!VIEWS.includes(name)) {
    return;
  }

  state.current = name;

  $$(".view").forEach(view => {
    view.classList.toggle(
      "active",
      view.dataset.view === name
    );
  });

  $$(".nav-btn").forEach(button => {
    button.classList.toggle(
      "active",
      button.dataset.target === name
    );
  });

  const index =
    VIEWS.indexOf(name);

  $("#progress").style.width =
    `${((index + 1) / VIEWS.length) * 100}%`;

  history.replaceState(
    null,
    "",
    "#" + name
  );

  const active =
    document.querySelector(
      `.view[data-view="${name}"]`
    );

  if (active) {
    active.scrollTop = 0;
    animateCounters(active);
  }
}

function openDrawer(
  type,
  title,
  value
) {

  $("#drawerType").textContent =
    type;

  $("#drawerTitle").textContent =
    title;

  $("#drawerMeta").textContent =
    "Verified snapshot projection";

  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML =
    `<pre>${esc(
      JSON.stringify(
        value,
        null,
        2
      )
    )}</pre>`;

  $("#drawer")
    .classList.add("open");
}

function closeDrawer() {
  $("#drawer")
    .classList.remove("open");
}

function renderMetrics(data) {

  const m =
    data.metrics || {};

  const max =
    Math.max(
      ...Object.values(m)
        .filter(Number.isFinite),
      1
    );

  const bar = value =>
    Math.max(
      7,
      Math.min(
        100,
        Math.log10(
          Number(value || 0) + 1
        ) /
        Math.log10(max + 1) *
        100
      )
    );

  $("#metrics").innerHTML = [
    metric(
      "Files",
      m.files,
      bar(m.files)
    ),
    metric(
      "Relationships",
      m.constitutional_relationships,
      bar(m.constitutional_relationships)
    ),
    metric(
      "Disciplines",
      m.kindred_disciplines,
      bar(m.kindred_disciplines)
    ),
    metric(
      "Tests discovered",
      m.test_functions,
      bar(m.test_functions)
    )
  ].join("");

  $("#runtimeMetrics").innerHTML = [
    metric(
      "Files",
      m.files,
      bar(m.files)
    ),
    metric(
      "Directories",
      m.directories,
      bar(m.directories)
    ),
    metric(
      "Authority records",
      m.authority_records,
      bar(m.authority_records)
    ),
    metric(
      "Exiles",
      m.exiles,
      bar(m.exiles)
    ),
    metric(
      "Relationships",
      m.constitutional_relationships,
      bar(m.constitutional_relationships)
    ),
    metric(
      "Disciplines",
      m.kindred_disciplines,
      bar(m.kindred_disciplines)
    ),
    metric(
      "Test files",
      m.test_files,
      bar(m.test_files)
    ),
    metric(
      "Test functions",
      m.test_functions,
      bar(m.test_functions)
    )
  ].join("");

  animateCounters();
}

function renderAuthority(data) {

  const statuses =
    data.authority_statuses || {};

  const total =
    Object.values(statuses)
      .reduce(
        (a,b) => a + Number(b || 0),
        0
      );

  $("#authoritySummary").textContent =
    `${fmt(total)} traced authority records`;

  $("#authorityPills").innerHTML =
    Object.entries(statuses)
      .map(([key,value]) => `
        <span class="pill">
          ${esc(key)} · ${fmt(value)}
        </span>
      `)
      .join("");
}

function renderedifice(data) {

  $("#edifice").innerHTML =
    (data.identity_edifice || [])
      .map((name,index) => `
        <div class="tier">

          <div class="tier-box">
            <div class="tier-name">
              ${esc(name)}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="tier-number">
            ${String(index + 1).padStart(2,"0")}
          </div>

        </div>
      `)
      .join("");
}

function createItem(
  index,
  title,
  copy,
  value,
  type,
  badge = ""
) {

  const element =
    document.createElement("article");

  element.className =
    "card item";

  element.innerHTML = `
    <div class="item-index">
      ${String(index + 1).padStart(2,"0")}
    </div>

    <h3>
      ${esc(title)}
    </h3>

    <p>
      ${esc(copy || "")}
    </p>

    ${
      badge
        ? `
          <span class="badge ${esc(badge)}">
            ${esc(badge)}
          </span>
        `
        : ""
    }
  `;

  element.addEventListener(
    "click",
    () => openDrawer(
      type,
      title,
      value
    )
  );

  return element;
}

function renderSubstrates(data) {

  const root =
    $("#substrates");

  root.innerHTML = "";

  (data.living_substrates || [])
    .forEach((item,index) => {

      const title =
        item.id ||
        item.name ||
        `Substrate ${index + 1}`;

      root.appendChild(
        createItem(
          index,
          titleCase(title),
          item.purpose || "",
          item,
          "Living Substrate"
        )
      );
    });
}

function renderDisciplines(data) {

  const root =
    $("#disciplines");

  root.innerHTML = "";

  (data.kindred_disciplines || [])
    .forEach((item,index) => {

      const title =
        item.canonical ||
        item.name ||
        item.id ||
        `Discipline ${index + 1}`;

      const relationCount =
        Array.isArray(item.relations)
          ? item.relations.length
          : 0;

      const copy =
        [
          relationCount
            ? `${relationCount} relationship forms`
            : "Relational discipline",
          item.steward || ""
        ]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(" · ");

      root.appendChild(
        createItem(
          index,
          titleCase(title),
          copy,
          item,
          "Kindred Discipline"
        )
      );
    });
}

function renderAuthorities(data) {

  const root =
    $("#authorities");

  root.innerHTML = "";

  (data.authority_records || [])
    .forEach((item,index) => {

      const title =
        item.title ||
        item.id ||
        item.path ||
        `Authority ${index + 1}`;

      const element =
        createItem(
          index,
          title,
          item.path || item.kind || "",
          item,
          "Authority Record",
          item.status || ""
        );

      root.appendChild(element);
    });
}

function renderAI(data) {

  const ai =
    data.ai || {};

  const mode =
    String(
      ai.mode || "native"
    ).toUpperCase();

  $("#aiMode").textContent =
    mode;

  $("#aiDescription").textContent =
    mode === "NATIVE"
      ? (
        "Savant is operating without external AI credentials. " +
        "Its native architecture remains valid and usable. " +
        "Configured providers can add capabilities without " +
        "changing the identity of the elements that consume them."
      )
      : (
        "External AI capability is active while Savant retains " +
        "native fallback. Provider activation augments execution " +
        "rather than becoming a new source of structural identity."
      );

  const providers =
    ai.providers || {};

  const root =
    $("#providers");

  if (!Object.keys(providers).length) {

    root.innerHTML = `
      <div class="provider">

        <div class="provider-dot"></div>

        <div class="provider-name">
          Provider registry
        </div>

        <div class="provider-state">
          native
        </div>

      </div>
    `;

    return;
  }

  root.innerHTML =
    Object.entries(providers)
      .map(([name,provider]) => `
        <div class="
          provider
          ${provider.usable ? "usable" : ""}
        ">

          <div class="provider-dot"></div>

          <div class="provider-name">
            ${esc(name)}
          </div>

          <div class="provider-state">
            ${
              provider.usable
                ? "active"
                : esc(
                    provider.reason ||
                    "inactive"
                  )
            }
          </div>

        </div>
      `)
      .join("");
}

function renderExiles(data) {

  const root =
    $("#exiles");

  root.innerHTML = "";

  (data.exiles || [])
    .forEach((item,index) => {

      const title =
        item.title ||
        item.name ||
        item.id ||
        `Exile ${index + 1}`;

      const purpose =
        Array.isArray(item.purpose)
          ? item.purpose.join(" · ")
          : item.purpose || "";

      root.appendChild(
        createItem(
          index,
          title,
          purpose,
          item,
          "Exile",
          item.status || ""
        )
      );
    });
}

function renderWhy(data) {

  const why =
    data.presentation?.why || [
      "To preserve meaning instead of merely storing output.",
      "To make complex systems composable instead of disposable.",
      "To keep authority, provenance and history visible.",
      "To let intelligence augment structure without owning it.",
      "To build something capable of becoming more than a collection of scripts."
    ];

  $("#whyList").innerHTML =
    why.map(line => `
      <div class="why-line">
        ${esc(line)}
      </div>
    `).join("");
}

function buildSearch(data) {

  const items = [];

  function add(
    type,
    title,
    value
  ) {

    if (!title) {
      return;
    }

    items.push({
      type,
      title: String(title),
      value
    });
  }

  (data.exiles || [])
    .forEach(item =>
      add(
        "Exile",
        item.title ||
        item.name ||
        item.id,
        item
      )
    );

  (data.authority_records || [])
    .forEach(item =>
      add(
        "Authority",
        item.title ||
        item.id ||
        item.path,
        item
      )
    );

  (data.kindred_disciplines || [])
    .forEach(item =>
      add(
        "Discipline",
        item.canonical ||
        item.name ||
        item.id,
        item
      )
    );

  (data.constitutional_relationships || [])
    .forEach(item =>
      add(
        "Relationship",
        item.name ||
        item.id ||
        item.canonical,
        item
      )
    );

  (data.living_substrates || [])
    .forEach(item =>
      add(
        "Substrate",
        item.id ||
        item.name,
        item
      )
    );

  state.search = items;
}

function showSearch() {

  $("#command")
    .classList.add("open");

  $("#commandInput").value =
    "";

  renderSearch("");

  setTimeout(
    () =>
      $("#commandInput").focus(),
    20
  );
}

function hideSearch() {
  $("#command")
    .classList.remove("open");
}

function renderSearch(query) {

  const q =
    query
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();

  const results =
    state.search
      .filter(item => {

        if (!q) {
          return true;
        }

        const corpus =
          `${item.title} ${item.type} ${
            JSON.stringify(item.value)
          }`
            .toLowerCase();

        return corpus.includes(q);
      })
      .slice(0,32);

  const root =
    $("#commandResults");

  root.innerHTML = "";

  results.forEach(item => {

    const row =
      document.createElement("div");

    row.className =
      "result";

    row.innerHTML = `
      <strong>
        ${esc(item.title)}
      </strong>

      <span>
        ${esc(item.type)}
      </span>
    `;

    row.addEventListener(
      "click",
      () => {
        hideSearch();

        openDrawer(
          item.type,
          item.title,
          item.value
        );
      }
    );

    root.appendChild(row);
  });
}

/* BACKGROUND FIELD */

function createSpace() {

  const canvas =
    $("#space");

  const ctx =
    canvas.getContext("2d");

  let width = 0;
  let height = 0;
  let stars = [];

  function resize() {

    const dpr =
      Math.min(
        window.devicePixelRatio || 1,
        2
      );

    width = innerWidth;
    height = innerHeight;

    canvas.width =
      width * dpr;

    canvas.height =
      height * dpr;

    canvas.style.width =
      width + "px";

    canvas.style.height =
      height + "px";

    ctx.setTransform(
      dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0
    );

    const count =
      Math.min(
        210,
        Math.max(
          70,
          Math.floor(
            width * height / 6500
          )
        )
      );

    stars =
      Array.from(
        {length: count},
        () => ({
          x: Math.random() * width,
          y: Math.random() * height,
          r: Math.random() * 1.15 + .15,
          a: Math.random() * .48 + .05,
          d: Math.random() * .15 + .02
        })
      );
  }

  let pointerX = 0;
  let pointerY = 0;

  addEventListener(
    "pointermove",
    event => {

      pointerX =
        event.clientX - width / 2;

      pointerY =
        event.clientY - height / 2;

      document.documentElement
        .style.setProperty(
          "--mx",
          `${event.clientX}px`
        );

      document.documentElement
        .style.setProperty(
          "--my",
          `${event.clientY}px`
        );
    },
    {passive: true}
  );

  function draw() {

    ctx.clearRect(
      0,0,width,height
    );

    for (const star of stars) {

      const x =
        star.x +
        pointerX * star.d * .025;

      const y =
        star.y +
        pointerY * star.d * .025;

      ctx.beginPath();

      ctx.arc(
        x,
        y,
        star.r,
        0,
        Math.PI * 2
      );

      ctx.fillStyle =
        `rgba(255,255,255,${star.a})`;

      ctx.fill();
    }

    requestAnimationFrame(draw);
  }

  resize();

  addEventListener(
    "resize",
    resize
  );

  draw();
}

/* INTERACTIVE TOPOLOGY */

function createGraph(data) {

  const canvas =
    $("#graph");

  const ctx =
    canvas.getContext("2d");

  let width = 0;
  let height = 0;
  let dpr = 1;

  const source =
    (data.exiles || [])
      .slice(0,18);

  const nodes =
    source.map(
      (item,index) => ({
        item,
        index,
        angle:
          Math.PI * 2 *
          index /
          Math.max(source.length,1),
        radius:
          .33 +
          (index % 3) * .055,
        x: 0,
        y: 0,
        vx: 0,
        vy: 0,
        hover: false
      })
    );

  let pointer = {
    x: -9999,
    y: -9999,
    down: false
  };

  function resize() {

    const rect =
      canvas.getBoundingClientRect();

    width = rect.width;
    height = rect.height;

    dpr =
      Math.min(
        window.devicePixelRatio || 1,
        2
      );

    canvas.width =
      width * dpr;

    canvas.height =
      height * dpr;

    ctx.setTransform(
      dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0
    );
  }

  function target(node,time) {

    const min =
      Math.min(width,height);

    const wobble =
      Math.sin(
        time * .00022 +
        node.index * 1.7
      ) * 7;

    return {
      x:
        width / 2 +
        Math.cos(
          node.angle +
          time * .000025 *
          (node.index % 2 ? 1 : -1)
        ) *
        min *
        node.radius +
        wobble,

      y:
        height / 2 +
        Math.sin(
          node.angle +
          time * .000025 *
          (node.index % 2 ? 1 : -1)
        ) *
        min *
        node.radius *
        .78
    };
  }

  function label(node) {

    return (
      node.item.title ||
      node.item.name ||
      node.item.id ||
      `Exile ${node.index + 1}`
    );
  }

  function draw(time) {

    ctx.clearRect(
      0,0,width,height
    );

    const cx =
      width / 2;

    const cy =
      height / 2;

    const min =
      Math.min(width,height);

    /* orbital fields */

    for (
      const factor of
      [.18,.31,.43]
    ) {

      ctx.beginPath();

      ctx.ellipse(
        cx,
        cy,
        min * factor,
        min * factor * .78,
        0,
        0,
        Math.PI * 2
      );

      ctx.strokeStyle =
        "rgba(255,255,255,.055)";

      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.stroke();
    }

    /* center */

    ctx.save();

    ctx.translate(cx,cy);

    ctx.rotate(
      Math.PI / 4 +
      time * .000035
    );

    ctx.strokeStyle =
      "rgba(217,181,109,.78)";

    ctx.lineWidth = 1;

    ctx.strokeRect(
      -44,
      -44,
      88,
      88
    );

    ctx.strokeStyle =
      "rgba(217,181,109,.25)";

    ctx.strokeRect(
      -27,
      -27,
      54,
      54
    );

    ctx.restore();

    /* update */

    nodes.forEach(node => {

      const t =
        target(node,time);

      node.vx +=
        (t.x - node.x) * .003;

      node.vy +=
        (t.y - node.y) * .003;

      node.vx *= .93;
      node.vy *= .93;

      node.x += node.vx;
      node.y += node.vy;

      if (
        !Number.isFinite(node.x) ||
        node.x === 0
      ) {
        node.x = t.x;
        node.y = t.y;
      }

      const dx =
        pointer.x - node.x;

      const dy =
        pointer.y - node.y;

      node.hover =
        Math.hypot(dx,dy) < 31;
    });

    /* graph edges */

    nodes.forEach((node,index) => {

      const next =
        nodes[
          (index + 1) %
          nodes.length
        ];

      if (!next) {
        return;
      }

      ctx.beginPath();

      ctx.moveTo(
        node.x,
        node.y
      );

      ctx.lineTo(
        next.x,
        next.y
      );

      ctx.strokeStyle =
        "rgba(217,181,109,.075)";

      ctx.stroke();

      ctx.beginPath();

      ctx.moveTo(
        cx,
        cy
      );

      ctx.lineTo(
        node.x,
        node.y
      );

      ctx.strokeStyle =
        node.hover
          ? "rgba(217,181,109,.34)"
          : "rgba(255,255,255,.045)";

      ctx.stroke();
    });

    /* nodes */

    nodes.forEach(node => {

      const active =
        node.hover;

      const r =
        active ? 25 : 21;

      ctx.beginPath();

      ctx.arc(
        node.x,
        node.y,
        r,
        0,
        Math.PI * 2
      );

      ctx.fillStyle =
        active
          ? "rgba(217,181,109,.12)"
          : "rgba(7,9,13,.88)";

      ctx.fill();

      ctx.strokeStyle =
        active
          ? "rgba(217,181,109,.85)"
          : "rgba(255,255,255,.16)";

      ctx.stroke();

      ctx.fillStyle =
        active
          ? "#f0d59b"
          : "#c5c7cc";

      ctx.font =
        "8px Inter, sans-serif";

      ctx.textAlign =
        "center";

      ctx.textBaseline =
        "middle";

      let text =
        label(node)
          .toUpperCase();

      if (text.length > 12) {
        text =
          text.slice(0,11) + "…";
      }

      ctx.fillText(
        text,
        node.x,
        node.y
      );
    });

    requestAnimationFrame(draw);
  }

  function point(event) {

    const rect =
      canvas.getBoundingClientRect();

    pointer.x =
      event.clientX - rect.left;

    pointer.y =
      event.clientY - rect.top;
  }

  canvas.addEventListener(
    "pointermove",
    event => {

      point(event);

      if (pointer.down) {

        nodes.forEach(node => {

          const dx =
            node.x - pointer.x;

          const dy =
            node.y - pointer.y;

          const distance =
            Math.max(
              30,
              Math.hypot(dx,dy)
            );

          if (distance < 120) {

            node.vx +=
              dx / distance * 1.8;

            node.vy +=
              dy / distance * 1.8;
          }
        });
      }

      canvas.style.cursor =
        nodes.some(
          node => node.hover
        )
          ? "pointer"
          : "crosshair";
    }
  );

  canvas.addEventListener(
    "pointerdown",
    event => {
      pointer.down = true;
      point(event);
    }
  );

  addEventListener(
    "pointerup",
    () => {
      pointer.down = false;
    }
  );

  canvas.addEventListener(
    "click",
    event => {

      point(event);

      const node =
        nodes.find(
          item =>
            Math.hypot(
              pointer.x - item.x,
              pointer.y - item.y
            ) < 32
        );

      if (node) {

        openDrawer(
          "Exile",
          label(node),
          node.item
        );
      }
    }
  );

  resize();

  addEventListener(
    "resize",
    resize
  );

  requestAnimationFrame(draw);

  state.graph = {
    nodes
  };
}

/* CARD LIGHTING */

function enableCardLighting() {

  document.addEventListener(
    "pointermove",
    event => {

      const card =
        event.target.closest(".card");

      if (!card) {
        return;
      }

      const rect =
        card.getBoundingClientRect();

      card.style.setProperty(
        "--card-x",
        `${event.clientX - rect.left}px`
      );

      card.style.setProperty(
        "--card-y",
        `${event.clientY - rect.top}px`
      );
    },
    {passive: true}
  );
}

  {
    view: "system",
    title: "Savant as a system",
    copy:
      "The Observatory begins with the measured runtime: topology, authority and scale."
  },
  {
    view: "architecture",
    title: "Recursive composition",
    copy:
      "Identity is organized hierarchically while reusable substrates describe behavior."
  },
  {
    view: "kindred",
    title: "Kindred",
    copy:
      "Relationships are promoted into reusable, graph-addressable infrastructure."
  },
  {
    view: "canon",
    title: "Canon",
    copy:
      "Authority remains traceable through status, source, lineage and provenance."
  },
  {
    view: "ai",
    title: "AI without dependency",
    copy:
      "AI capability augments Savant. The architecture remains valid without provider credentials."
  },
  {
    view: "runtime",
    title: "Measured implementation",
    copy:
      "The numbers shown here are generated from the runtime snapshot rather than typed into the presentation."
  },
  {
    view: "why",
    title: "Why it exists",
    copy:
      "Savant is an attempt to make complex creative and computational systems retain meaning, history and extensibility."
  }
];

function stopTour() {

  state.tour = false;

  clearTimeout(
    state.tourTimer
  );

  $("#tourBtn").textContent =
    "Tour";

  $("#tourToast")
    .classList.remove("show");
}

function startTour() {

  if (state.tour) {
    stopTour();
    return;
  }

  state.tour = true;

  $("#tourBtn").textContent =
    "Stop";

  let index = 0;

  function step() {

    if (!state.tour) {
      return;
    }

    const item =
      TOUR[index];

    navigate(item.view);

    $("#tourTitle").textContent =
      item.title;

    $("#tourCopy").textContent =
      item.copy;

    $("#tourToast")
      .classList.add("show");

    index++;

    if (index >= TOUR.length) {

      state.tourTimer =
        setTimeout(
          stopTour,
          7000
        );

      return;
    }

    state.tourTimer =
      setTimeout(
        step,
        7000
      );
  }

  step();
}

/* FULLSCREEN */

async function toggleFullscreen() {

  try {

    if (!document.fullscreenElement) {

      await document.documentElement
        .requestFullscreen();

      $("#fullBtn").textContent =
        "Exit";
    } else {

      await document
        .exitFullscreen();

      $("#fullBtn").textContent =
        "Fullscreen";
    }

  } catch (_) {
    /* browser may deny fullscreen */
  }
}



/* Spatial Observatory — deterministic presentation projection */
class SpatialObservatory {
  constructor(canvas){this.canvas=canvas;this.ctx=canvas.getContext("2d");this.nodes=[];this.edges=[];this.rotX=-.28;this.rotY=.45;this.zoom=1;this.drag=false;this.last=[0,0];this.selected=null;this.isolated=null;this.lens="structure";this.auto=false;this.running=false;this.bind();}
  build(data){
    const edifice=(data.identity_edifice||[]).map((x,i)=>({id:`identity:${typeof x==="string"?x:(x.name||x.id||i)}`,label:titleCase(typeof x==="string"?x:(x.name||x.id||`tier ${i}`)),kind:"identity",raw:x,depth:i+1}));
    const disciplines=(data.kindred_disciplines||[]).slice(0,12).map((x,i)=>({id:`kindred:${typeof x==="string"?x:(x.name||x.id||i)}`,label:titleCase(typeof x==="string"?x:(x.name||x.id||`discipline ${i}`)),kind:"kindred",raw:x,depth:5}));
    const statuses=(data.authority_statuses||[]).slice(0,8).map((x,i)=>({id:`authority:${typeof x==="string"?x:(x.name||x.id||i)}`,label:titleCase(typeof x==="string"?x:(x.name||x.id||`status ${i}`)),kind:"authority",raw:x,depth:3}));
    const substrates=(data.living_substrates||[]).slice(0,8).map((x,i)=>({id:`substrate:${typeof x==="string"?x:(x.name||x.id||i)}`,label:titleCase(typeof x==="string"?x:(x.name||x.id||`substrate ${i}`)),kind:"substrate",raw:x,depth:4}));
    const root={id:"savant",label:"SAVANT",kind:"root",raw:{schema:data.schema,read_only:data.read_only,snapshot_sha256:data.snapshot_sha256},depth:0};
    this.nodes=[root,...edifice,...disciplines,...statuses,...substrates];
    const rings={identity:135,authority:215,kindred:300,substrate:375}; const groups={}; this.nodes.slice(1).forEach(n=>(groups[n.kind]??=[]).push(n));
    Object.entries(groups).forEach(([kind,list])=>list.forEach((n,i)=>{const a=(i/list.length)*Math.PI*2+(kind==="kindred"?.35:0);const r=rings[kind]||250;n.x=Math.cos(a)*r;n.z=Math.sin(a)*r;n.y=(i%3-1)*58+(kind==="identity"?-35:kind==="authority"?30:0);})); root.x=root.y=root.z=0;
    this.edges=[]; edifice.forEach((n,i)=>this.edges.push({a:i?edifice[i-1]:root,b:n,type:"lineage"})); disciplines.forEach(n=>this.edges.push({a:root,b:n,type:"relationship"})); statuses.forEach(n=>this.edges.push({a:root,b:n,type:"authority"})); substrates.forEach((n,i)=>this.edges.push({a:edifice[Math.min(edifice.length-1,Math.max(0,i%Math.max(1,edifice.length)))]||root,b:n,type:"projection"}));
    this.updateReadout();this.renderStory();if(!this.running){this.running=true;requestAnimationFrame(t=>this.frame(t));}
  }
  bind(){
    const pos=e=>{const r=this.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();return[(e.clientX-r.left)*(this.canvas.width/r.width),(e.clientY-r.top)*(this.canvas.height/r.height)]};
    this.canvas.addEventListener("pointerdown",e=>{this.drag=true;this.last=[e.clientX,e.clientY];this.canvas.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId)});
    this.canvas.addEventListener("pointermove",e=>{if(!this.drag)return;this.rotY+=(e.clientX-this.last[0])*.006;this.rotX+=(e.clientY-this.last[1])*.004;this.rotX=Math.max(-1.2,Math.min(1.2,this.rotX));this.last=[e.clientX,e.clientY]});
    this.canvas.addEventListener("pointerup",e=>{if(this.drag){const dx=Math.abs(e.clientX-this.last[0]),dy=Math.abs(e.clientY-this.last[1]);if(dx<4&&dy<4)this.pick(...pos(e));}this.drag=false});
    this.canvas.addEventListener("wheel",e=>{e.preventDefault();this.zoom=Math.max(.55,Math.min(2.4,this.zoom*(e.deltaY>0?.92:1.08)))},{passive:false});
    this.canvas.addEventListener("dblclick",e=>{this.pick(...pos(e));if(this.selected)this.isolated=this.isolated===this.selected.id?null:this.selected.id;this.updateReadout()});
    document.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Escape"&&this.isolated){this.isolated=null;this.updateReadout()}});
    document.addEventListener("click",e=>{const l=e.target.closest?.("[data-lens]");if(l){this.lens=l.dataset.lens;$$('.lens-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b===l));this.updateReadout();}const c=e.target.closest?.("[data-camera]");if(c){const m=c.dataset.camera;if(m==='reset'){this.rotX=-.28;this.rotY=.45;this.zoom=1;this.auto=false}else this.auto=m==='auto';$$('.camera-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',(b.dataset.camera==='auto')===this.auto && b.dataset.camera!=='reset'));}});
  }
  project(n,w,h){let x=n.x||0,y=n.y||0,z=n.z||0;const cy=Math.cos(this.rotY),sy=Math.sin(this.rotY),cx=Math.cos(this.rotX),sx=Math.sin(this.rotX);let x1=x*cy-z*sy,z1=x*sy+z*cy;let y1=y*cx-z1*sx,z2=y*sx+z1*cx;const f=720/(720+z2);return{x:w/2+x1*f*this.zoom,y:h/2+y1*f*this.zoom,z:z2,f};}
  visible(n){if(!this.isolated)return true;if(n.id===this.isolated||n.id==='savant')return true;return this.edges.some(e=>(e.a.id===this.isolated&&e.b.id===n.id)||(e.b.id===this.isolated&&e.a.id===n.id));}
  color(n){return n.kind==='root'?'#f0d59b':n.kind==='identity'?'#d9b56d':n.kind==='authority'?'#8dd3a6':n.kind==='kindred'?'#aaa3ef':'#9ab6d8'}
  frame(t){if(this.auto)this.rotY+=.0018;this.draw();requestAnimationFrame(x=>this.frame(x));}
  draw(){const c=this.canvas,ctx=this.ctx,dpr=Math.min(devicePixelRatio||1,2),r=c.getBoundingClientRect(),w=Math.max(1,Math.floor(r.width*dpr)),h=Math.max(1,Math.floor(r.height*dpr));if(c.width!==w||c.height!==h){c.width=w;c.height=h}ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);const pts=new Map(this.nodes.map(n=>[n.id,this.project(n,w,h)]));
    this.edges.forEach(e=>{if(!this.visible(e.a)||!this.visible(e.b))return;if(this.lens==='lineage'&&e.type!=='lineage')return;if(this.lens==='authority'&&e.type!=='authority')return;if(this.lens==='projection'&&e.type!=='projection')return;if(this.lens==='dependency'&&e.type==='authority')return;const a=pts.get(e.a.id),b=pts.get(e.b.id);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(a.x,a.y);ctx.lineTo(b.x,b.y);ctx.strokeStyle=e.type==='lineage'?'rgba(217,181,109,.34)':e.type==='authority'?'rgba(141,211,166,.28)':e.type==='projection'?'rgba(154,182,216,.25)':'rgba(170,163,239,.18)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,dpr*.7);ctx.stroke();});
    [...this.nodes].filter(n=>this.visible(n)).sort((a,b)=>pts.get(b.id).z-pts.get(a.id).z).forEach(n=>{const p=pts.get(n.id),sel=this.selected?.id===n.id,rad=(n.kind==='root'?17:7)*p.f*dpr;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,Math.max(3,rad),0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fillStyle=this.color(n);ctx.globalAlpha=sel?1:.72;ctx.fill();ctx.globalAlpha=1;if(sel){ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,rad+9*dpr,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(240,213,155,.55)';ctx.stroke()}if(n.kind==='root'||sel||p.f>1.08){ctx.font=`${Math.max(8,9*p.f*dpr)}px Inter, sans-serif`;ctx.fillStyle='rgba(244,242,237,.72)';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText(n.label,p.x,p.y-rad-7*dpr)}});
    this._pts=pts;
  }
  pick(x,y){let best=null,dist=26*(devicePixelRatio||1);this.nodes.filter(n=>this.visible(n)).forEach(n=>{const p=this._pts?.get(n.id);if(!p)return;const d=Math.hypot(p.x-x,p.y-y);if(d<dist){dist=d;best=n}});if(best){this.selected=best;this.inspect(best)}}
  inspect(n){$('#spatialTitle').textContent=n.label;$('#spatialCopy').textContent=n.kind==='root'?'The loaded snapshot is the presentation source. The Observatory is disposable and read-only; it visualizes structure without acquiring authority.':`This ${n.kind} node participates in Savant through addressable relationships rather than duplicated ownership. Select a lens to change which relation class is emphasized.`;$('#spatialTags').innerHTML=[n.kind,`depth ${n.depth}`,'inspectable','projection'].map(x=>`<span>${esc(x)}</span>`).join('');}
  updateReadout(){const visible=this.nodes.filter(n=>this.visible(n));$('#spatialNodeCount').textContent=visible.length;$('#spatialEdgeCount').textContent=this.edges.filter(e=>this.visible(e.a)&&this.visible(e.b)).length;$('#spatialDepth').textContent=Math.max(0,...visible.map(n=>n.depth||0));$('#spatialLens').textContent=titleCase(this.lens);$('#spatialMode').textContent=`${titleCase(this.lens)} lens · ${this.isolated?'isolated subgraph':'all nodes'}`;}
  renderStory(){const steps=[['Instances are substance','Authoritative primitives are instantiated once; higher structures emerge by composition.'],['Segues carry interaction','Relationships are first-class edges instead of hidden direct coupling.'],['Lineage is directional','Parent and child views derive from directed lineage rather than duplicated inverse ownership.'],['Authority stays distinct','Canonical, accepted, provisional and other statuses remain visible rather than flattened.'],['Projection is disposable','UI, indexes, reports and generated files can be rebuilt from authoritative sources.'],['Dependencies are traversable','Upstream and downstream impact can be inspected without turning the index into authority.'],['Extension avoids replacement','Identity, provenance and attachment points survive evolution instead of being erased.']];$('#spatialStory').innerHTML=steps.map((x,i)=>`<div class="story-step ${i===0?'active':''}" data-story="${i}"><strong>${x[0]}</strong><span>${x[1]}</span></div>`).join('');}
}
let spatialObservatory=null;
function renderSpatial(data){const c=$('#spatialCanvas');if(!c)return;spatialObservatory=spatialObservatory||new SpatialObservatory(c);spatialObservatory.build(data);}

/* RENDER */

function render(data) {

  state.data =
    data;

  const metrics =
    data.metrics || {};

  $("#runtimeState").textContent =
    `Verified snapshot · ${
      fmt(metrics.files)
    } files`;

  $("#snapshotHash").textContent =
    "SHA · " +
    String(
      data.snapshot_sha256 || ""
    ).slice(0,16);

  renderMetrics(data);
  renderSpatial(data);
  renderAuthority(data);
  renderedifice(data);
  renderSubstrates(data);
  renderDisciplines(data);
  renderAuthorities(data);
  renderAI(data);
  renderExiles(data);
  renderWhy(data);
  buildSearch(data);
  createGraph(data);

  const requested =
    location.hash
      .replace("#","");

  if (VIEWS.includes(requested)) {
    navigate(requested);
  } else {
    navigate("system");
  }
}

        $("#command")

        event.target.value

document.addEventListener(
  "keydown",
  event => {

    const typing =
      ["INPUT","TEXTAREA"]
        .includes(
          document.activeElement.tagName
        );

    if (
      event.key === "/" &&
      !typing
    ) {

      event.preventDefault();
      showSearch();
      return;
    }

    if (
      (event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) &&
      event.key.toLowerCase() === "k"
    ) {

      event.preventDefault();
      showSearch();
      return;
    }

    if (event.key === "Escape") {

      hideSearch();
      closeDrawer();
      stopTour();

      return;
    }

    if (
      !typing &&
      event.key >= "1" &&
      event.key <= "7"
    ) {

      navigate(
        VIEWS[
          Number(event.key) - 1
        ]
      );
    }

    if (
      !typing &&
      event.key === "ArrowRight"
    ) {

      const index =
        VIEWS.indexOf(
          state.current
        );

      navigate(
        VIEWS[
          Math.min(
            VIEWS.length - 1,
            index + 1
          )
        ]
      );
    }

    if (
      !typing &&
      event.key === "ArrowLeft"
    ) {

      const index =
        VIEWS.indexOf(
          state.current
        );

      navigate(
        VIEWS[
          Math.max(
            0,
            index - 1
          )
        ]
      );
    }
  }
);

/* INIT */

async function init() {

  createSpace();
  enableCardLighting();

  try {

    const response =
      await fetch(
        "../data/savant.snapshot.json",
        {
          cache: "no-store"
        }
      );

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(
        `HTTP ${response.status}`
      );
    }

    const data =
      await response.json();

    render(data);

  } catch (error) {

    console.error(error);

    $("#error")
      .classList.add("show");
  }
}

init();
</script>

/* FOUNDER'S DEMONSTRATION CONTROLLER v3 */
(function(){

Higher structures emerge by composition, attachment, specialization and projection rather than copying lower-level substance.",m:["9 identity tiers","9 living substrates"]}),

    ()=>({view:"canon",k:"04 · Memory",t:"The system remembers how it became true.",c:why(1,"Savant preserves history as it evolves.")+" "+why(2,"Authority, lineage and provenance remain visible."),m:["canonical ≠ accepted ≠ provisional","supersession preserved"]}),
    ()=>({view:"ai",k:"05 · Intelligence",t:"AI is capability, not authority.",c:why(3,"Savant remains useful without an external AI provider and can become more capable when providers are available."),m:[`mode: ${(data()?.ai?.mode||"native")}`,"provider state is explicit"]}),
    ()=>({view:"runtime",k:"06 · Specialization",t:"Many owners. One composable system.",c:`The runtime currently projects ${metric("exiles")} Exiles. Each owns a bounded semantic responsibility while sharing reusable constitutional and relational machinery.`,m:["ownership remains explicit","extensions do not require replacement"]}),
    ()=>({view:"why",k:"07 · Purpose",t:"Why build Savant?",c:why(0,"To build complex intelligent software while preserving where knowledge came from.")+" "+why(4,"To make an ambitious system composable instead of brittle."),m:["lineage","provenance","replay","extensibility"]}),
    ()=>({view:"why",k:"08 · Dedication",t:p().dedication||"For Mom.",c:"This demonstration is a projection of the work itself: a system designed to grow without erasing its history, and to make the structure behind that growth visible.",m:[p().edition||"Founder's Demonstration",p().date||"August 2026"],final:true})
  ];

  function goView(v){
    if(typeof window.navigate==="function") window.navigate(v);
    else{
      document.querySelectorAll(".view").forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle("active",x.dataset.view===v));
      document.querySelectorAll(".nav-btn").forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle("active",x.dataset.target===v));
    }
  }
  function render(){
    const s=slides[index]();
    goView(s.view);
    $d("#demoKicker").textContent=s.k;
    $d("#demoTitle").textContent=s.t;
    $d("#demoTitle").classList.toggle("demo-final",!!s.final);
    $d("#demoCopy").textContent=s.c;
    $d("#demoMeta").innerHTML=s.m.map(x=>`<span>${String(x)}</span>`).join("<span>·</span>");
    $d("#demoProgress").style.width=((index+1)/slides.length*100)+"%";
    $d("#demoPrev").disabled=index===0;
    $d("#demoNext").textContent=index===slides.length-1?"Finish":"Next";
    schedule();
  }
  function schedule(){
    clearTimeout(timer);
    if(paused) return;
    timer=setTimeout(()=>{if(index<slides.length-1){index++;render()}else stop()},duration);
  }
  function start(){
    if(!data()) return;
    if(typeof window.stopTour==="function") window.stopTour();
    index=0;paused=false;
    document.body.classList.add("demo-mode");
    hud.classList.add("show");
    $d("#demoPause").textContent="Pause";

  }
  function stop(){
    clearTimeout(timer);
    hud.classList.remove("show");
    document.body.classList.remove("demo-mode");
    paused=false;
  }
  function next(){if(index<slides.length-1){index++;render()}else stop()}
  function prev(){if(index>0){index--;render()}}
  function pause(){paused=!paused;$d("#demoPause").textContent=paused?"Resume":"Pause";schedule()}

  const demoButton=$d("#demoBtn");
  if(demoButton){
    demoButton.addEventListener("click",()=>{
      if(!data()){
        const toast=$d("#toast");
        if(toast){ toast.textContent="Runtime snapshot is still loading."; toast.classList.add("show"); setTimeout(()=>toast.classList.remove("show"),1800); }
        return;
      }
      start();
    });
  }
  $d("#demoNext").addEventListener("click",next);
  $d("#demoPrev").addEventListener("click",prev);
  $d("#demoPause").addEventListener("click",pause);
  $d("#demoExit").addEventListener("click",stop);
  document.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{
    if(!hud.classList.contains("show")) return;
    if(e.key==="Escape"){e.preventDefault();stop()}
    else if(e.key==="ArrowRight"){e.preventDefault();next()}
    else if(e.key==="ArrowLeft"){e.preventDefault();prev()}
    else if(e.key===" "){e.preventDefault();pause()}
  });
  window.startDemonstration=start;
})();

#!/usr/bin/env python3

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import http.server
import socketserver
from pathlib import Path


ROOT = Path("/root/savant-runtime/present").resolve()


class ObservatoryHandler(
    http.server.SimpleHTTPRequestHandler
):
    def __init__(
        self,
        *args,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        super().__init__(
            *args,
            directory=str(ROOT),
            **kwargs,
        )

    def end_headers(self) -> None:
        self.send_header(
            "Cache-Control",
            "no-store",
        )
        self.send_header(
            "X-Content-Type-Options",
            "nosniff",
        )
        self.send_header(
            "X-Frame-Options",
            "SAMEORIGIN",
        )
        self.send_header(
            "Referrer-Policy",
            "no-referrer",
        )
        super().end_headers()

    def log_message(
        self,
        format: str,
        *args,
    ) -> None:
        return


class ReusableTCPServer(
    socketserver.ThreadingTCPServer
):
    allow_reuse_address = True
    daemon_threads = True


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Serve the Savant Observatory."
        )
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--host",
        default="127.0.0.1",
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--port",
        type=int,
        default=8765,
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    with ReusableTCPServer(
        (args.host, args.port),
        ObservatoryHandler,
    ) as server:
        print(
            "Savant Observatory: "
            f"http://{args.host}:{args.port}/web/"
        )

        server.serve_forever()

    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
