def stable_json(value: Any) -> str:
    return json.dumps(
        value,
        ensure_ascii=False,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    )


def digest(value: Any) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(
        stable_json(value).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()


def read_yaml(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    try:
        import yaml
    except ImportError:
        return None

    try:
        with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            value = yaml.safe_load(handle)
    except (OSError, UnicodeError, ValueError):
        return None

    return value if isinstance(value, dict) else None


def read_json(path: Path) -> Any:
    try:
        with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            return json.load(handle)
    except (OSError, UnicodeError, ValueError):
        return None


def count_files(root: Path) -> int:
    if not root.exists():
        return 0

    return sum(
        1
        for path in root.rglob("*")
        if path.is_file()
    )


def count_dirs(root: Path) -> int:
    if not root.exists():
        return 0

    return sum(
        1
        for path in root.rglob("*")
        if path.is_dir()
    )


def extension_counts(root: Path) -> dict[str, int]:
    counter: Counter[str] = Counter()

    if not root.exists():
        return {}

    for path in root.rglob("*"):
        if not path.is_file():
            continue

        suffix = path.suffix.lower() or "[none]"
        counter[suffix] += 1

    return dict(
        sorted(
            counter.items(),
            key=lambda item: (
                -item[1],
                item[0],
            ),
        )
    )


def authority_records() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    authority_root = (
        SAVANT
        / "canon-system"
        / "authority"
    )

    if not authority_root.exists():
        return records

    for path in sorted(
        authority_root.rglob("*.yaml")
    ):
        value = read_yaml(path)

        if not value:
            continue

        records.append(
            {
                "path": str(
                    path.relative_to(SAVANT)
                ),
                "id": value.get("id"),
                "kind": value.get("kind"),
                "status": value.get("status"),
                "title": value.get("title"),
                "authority": value.get(
                    "authority",
                    {},
                ),
                "dependencies": value.get(
                    "dependencies",
                    [],
                ),
                "relationships": value.get(
                    "relationships",
                    [],
                ),
                "lineage": value.get(
                    "lineage",
                    {},
                ),
                "provenance": value.get(
                    "provenance",
                    {},
                ),
            }
        )

    return records


def exile_records() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    if not EXILES.exists():
        return records

    for path in sorted(
        EXILES.glob("*.yaml")
    ):
        value = read_yaml(path)

        if not value:
            continue

        purpose = value.get(
            "purpose",
            {},
        )

        if isinstance(purpose, dict):
            current_purpose = purpose.get(
                "current",
                [],
            )
        else:
            current_purpose = purpose

        orchestration = value.get(
            "orchestration",
            {},
        )

        if not isinstance(
            orchestration,
            dict,
        ):
            orchestration = {}

        authority = value.get(
            "authority",
            {},
        )

        if not isinstance(
            authority,
            dict,
        ):
            authority = {}

        records.append(
            {
                "id": value.get(
                    "id",
                    f"exile:{path.stem}",
                ),
                "name": path.stem,
                "title": value.get(
                    "title",
                    path.stem,
                ),
                "status": value.get(
                    "status",
                    "unknown",
                ),
                "purpose": current_purpose,
                "authority_state": authority.get(
                    "state",
                    "unknown",
                ),
                "confidence": authority.get(
                    "confidence"
                ),
                "opus_ready": bool(
                    orchestration.get(
                        "opus_ready"
                    )
                ),
                "chain_alias": orchestration.get(
                    "chain_alias"
                ),
                "dependencies": value.get(
                    "dependencies",
                    [],
                ),
                "relationships": value.get(
                    "relationships",
                    [],
                ),
                "lineage": value.get(
                    "lineage",
                    {},
                ),
                "provenance": value.get(
                    "provenance",
                    {},
                ),
            }
        )

    return records


def constitutional_relationships() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    path = (
        CONSTITUTION
        / "relationships.json"
    )

    payload = read_json(path)

    if not isinstance(
        payload,
        dict,
    ):
        return []

    relationships = payload.get(
        "relationships",
        [],
    )

    if not isinstance(
        relationships,
        list,
    ):
        return []

    return [
        dict(item)
        for item in relationships
        if isinstance(item, dict)
    ]


def kindred_disciplines() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    path = (
        KINDRED
        / "discipline_registry.yaml"
    )

    value = read_yaml(path)

    if not value:
        return []

    disciplines = value.get(
        "disciplines",
        [],
    )

    if not isinstance(
        disciplines,
        list,
    ):
        return []

    return [
        dict(item)
        for item in disciplines
        if isinstance(item, dict)
    ]


def run_json_command(
    command: list[str],
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            command,
            check=False,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=15,
            env=os.environ.copy(),
        )
    except (
        OSError,
        subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
    ):
        return None

    if result.returncode != 0:
        return None

    try:
        value = json.loads(
            result.stdout
        )
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return None

    return (
        value
        if isinstance(value, dict)
        else None
    )


def ai_status() -> dict[str, Any]:
    command = (
        SAVANT
        / "commands"
        / "savant-ai"
    )

    if not command.exists():
        return {
            "available": False,
            "mode": "unknown",
        }

    value = run_json_command(
        [
            str(command),
            "status",
        ]
    )

    if value is None:
        return {
            "available": False,
            "mode": "unknown",
        }

    providers = value.get(
        "providers",
        {},
    )

    safe_providers: dict[str, Any] = {}

    if isinstance(
        providers,
        dict,
    ):
        for name, provider in providers.items():
            if not isinstance(
                provider,
                dict,
            ):
                continue

            safe_providers[name] = {
                "configured": bool(
                    provider.get(
                        "configured"
                    )
                ),
                "usable": bool(
                    provider.get(
                        "usable"
                    )
                ),
                "capabilities": provider.get(
                    "capabilities",
                    [],
                ),
                "reason": provider.get(
                    "reason",
                ),
            }

    return {
        "available": True,
        "mode": value.get(
            "mode",
            "unknown",
        ),
        "usable_providers": value.get(
            "usable_providers",
            [],
        ),
        "capabilities": value.get(
            "capabilities",
            [],
        ),
        "providers": safe_providers,
    }


def test_inventory() -> dict[str, int]:
    tests_root = SAVANT / "tests"

    if not tests_root.exists():
        return {
            "files": 0,
            "test_functions": 0,
        }

    files = 0
    functions = 0

    for path in tests_root.rglob(
        "test*.py"
    ):
        files += 1

        try:
            text = path.read_text(
                encoding="utf-8",
                errors="replace",
            )
        except OSError:
            continue

        functions += sum(
            1
            for line in text.splitlines()
            if line.lstrip().startswith(
                "def test"
            )
        )

    return {
        "files": files,
        "test_functions": functions,
    }


def build() -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not SAVANT.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Savant runtime missing: {SAVANT}"
        )

    authorities = authority_records()
    exiles = exile_records()
    relationships = (
        constitutional_relationships()
    )
    disciplines = kindred_disciplines()
    tests = test_inventory()

    status_counter = Counter(
        str(
            record.get(
                "status",
                "unknown",
            )
        )
        for record in authorities
    )

    kind_counter = Counter(
        str(
            record.get(
                "kind",
                "unknown",
            )
        )
        for record in authorities
    )

    snapshot: dict[str, Any] = {
        "schema": (
            "savant.observatory.snapshot.v1"
        ),
        "generated_at": datetime.now(
            timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
        "source_root": str(SAVANT),
        "presentation_root": str(
            PRESENT
        ),
        "read_only": True,
        "identity_edifice": (
            IDENTITY_edifice
        ),
        "living_substrates": (
            LIVING_SUBSTRATES
        ),
        "ownership": OWNERS,
        "metrics": {
            "files": count_files(
                SAVANT
            ),
            "directories": count_dirs(
                SAVANT
            ),
            "authority_records": len(
                authorities
            ),
            "exiles": len(
                exiles
            ),
            "constitutional_relationships": len(
                relationships
            ),
            "kindred_disciplines": len(
                disciplines
            ),
            "test_files": tests[
                "files"
            ],
            "test_functions": tests[
                "test_functions"
            ],
        },
        "extensions": extension_counts(
            SAVANT
        ),
        "authority_statuses": dict(
            sorted(
                status_counter.items()
            )
        ),
        "authority_kinds": dict(
            sorted(
                kind_counter.items()
            )
        ),
        "exiles": exiles,
        "authority_records": authorities,
        "constitutional_relationships": (
            relationships
        ),
        "kindred_disciplines": (
            disciplines
        ),
        "ai": ai_status(),
        "presentation": {
            "title": "SAVANT",
            "subtitle": (
                "A recursively composable "
                "intelligence architecture"
            ),
            "edition": (
                "Founder's Demonstration"
            ),
            "date": "August 2026",
            "dedication": "For Mom.",
            "why": [
                (
                    "To build complex intelligent "
                    "software that can preserve "
                    "where its knowledge came from."
                ),
                (
                    "To let a system evolve without "
                    "destroying its history."
                ),
                (
                    "To make relationships, "
                    "dependencies, authority, "
                    "lineage and provenance visible."
                ),
                (
                    "To remain useful without an "
                    "external AI provider while "
                    "becoming more capable when "
                    "providers are available."
                ),
                (
                    "To make an ambitious system "
                    "composable instead of brittle."
                ),
            ],
        },
    }

    snapshot[
        "snapshot_sha256"
    ] = digest(snapshot)

    return snapshot


def main() -> int:
    try:
        snapshot = build()

        OUTPUT.parent.mkdir(
            parents=True,
            exist_ok=True,
        )

        OUTPUT.write_text(
            json.dumps(
                snapshot,
                indent=2,
                ensure_ascii=False,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
            + "\n",
            encoding="utf-8",
        )

        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "valid": True,
                    "output": str(
                        OUTPUT
                    ),
                    "sha256": snapshot[
                        "snapshot_sha256"
                    ],
                    "metrics": snapshot[
                        "metrics"
                    ],
                },
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )

        return 0

    except Exception as exc:
        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "valid": False,
                    "error": str(exc),
                },
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            ),
            file=sys.stderr,
        )

        return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

================================================================================
FILE: /root/savant-runtime/present/tools/present_dump.py
SHA256: acc24cc03006644d916d84baa96c00405aa6c11aaef5bce23e20eaa15ef99364
================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json
import os
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone


ROOT = Path("/root/savant-runtime/present")
OUTPUT = Path("/root/savant-runtime/present/present-current-dump.txt")


INCLUDE_SUFFIXES = {
    ".html",
    ".css",
    ".js",
    ".json",
    ".md",
    ".txt",
    ".py",
    ".conf",
}

EXCLUDE_NAMES = {
    "present-current-dump.txt",
}

MAX_FILE_BYTES = 2 * 1024 * 1024


def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()

    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)

    return digest.hexdigest()


def eligible(path: Path) -> bool:
    if not path.is_file():
        return False

    if path.name in EXCLUDE_NAMES:
        return False

    if path.suffix.lower() not in INCLUDE_SUFFIXES:
        return False

    try:
        return path.stat().st_size <= MAX_FILE_BYTES
    except OSError:
        return False


def main() -> int:
    if not ROOT.is_dir():
        print(f"ERROR: missing root: {ROOT}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    files = sorted(
        (path for path in ROOT.rglob("*") if eligible(path)),
        key=lambda path: str(path.relative_to(ROOT)),
    )

    manifest = []

    for path in files:
        stat = path.stat()

        manifest.append(
            {
                "path": str(path),
                "relative_path": str(path.relative_to(ROOT)),
                "bytes": stat.st_size,
                "mode": oct(stat.st_mode & 0o7777),
                "sha256": sha256(path),
            }
        )

    with OUTPUT.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as output:
        output.write("SAVANT PRESENT CURRENT IMPLEMENTATION DUMP\n")
        output.write(
            "generated_utc="
            + datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
            + "\n"
        )
        output.write(f"root={ROOT}\n")
        output.write(f"file_count={len(files)}\n\n")

        output.write("=== MANIFEST ===\n")
        output.write(
            json.dumps(
                manifest,
                indent=2,
                ensure_ascii=False,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )
        output.write("\n\n")

        for path in files:
            output.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
            output.write(f"FILE: {path}\n")
            output.write(f"SHA256: {sha256(path)}\n")
            output.write("=" * 80 + "\n")

            try:
                text = path.read_text(
                    encoding="utf-8",
                    errors="replace",
                )
            except OSError as exc:
                output.write(f"[READ ERROR: {exc}]\n\n")
                continue

            output.write(text)

            if not text.endswith("\n"):
                output.write("\n")

            output.write("\n")

    print(f"WROTE: {OUTPUT}")
    print(f"FILES: {len(files)}")
    print(f"BYTES: {OUTPUT.stat().st_size}")
    print(f"SHA256: {sha256(OUTPUT)}")

    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

<script>
"use strict";

const VIEWS = [
  "system",
  "architecture",
  "kindred",
  "canon",
  "ai",
  "runtime",
  "why"
];

const state = {
  data: null,
  search: [],
  current: "system",
  tour: false,
  tourTimer: null,
  graph: null
};

const $ = selector =>
  document.querySelector(selector);

const $$ = selector =>
  [...document.querySelectorAll(selector)];

const esc = value =>
  String(value ?? "")
    .replaceAll("&","&amp;")
    .replaceAll("<","&lt;")
    .replaceAll(">","&gt;")
    .replaceAll('"',"&quot;");

const fmt = value =>
  Number(value || 0)
    .toLocaleString();

function titleCase(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .replaceAll("_"," ")
    .replaceAll("-"," ")
    .replace(
      /\b\w/g,
      letter => letter.toUpperCase()
    );
}

function metric(
  label,
  value,
  bar = 50
) {
  return `
    <div
      class="card metric"
      style="--bar:${bar}%"
    >
      <div
        class="metric-value"
        data-count="${Number(value || 0)}"
      >
        0
      </div>

      <div class="metric-label">
        ${esc(label)}
      </div>

      <div class="metric-bar"></div>
    </div>
  `;
}

function animateCounters(root = document) {
  root
    .querySelectorAll("[data-count]")
    .forEach(element => {

      const target =
        Number(element.dataset.count || 0);

      const start =
        performance.now();

      const duration =
        Math.min(
          1300,
          500 +
          Math.log10(target + 1) * 170
        );

      function frame(now) {

        const t =
          Math.min(
            1,
            (now - start) / duration
          );

        const eased =
          1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 4);

        element.textContent =
          Math.floor(
            target * eased
          ).toLocaleString();

        if (t < 1) {
          requestAnimationFrame(frame);
        }
      }

      requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    });
}

function navigate(name) {

  if (!VIEWS.includes(name)) {
    return;
  }

  state.current = name;

  $$(".view").forEach(view => {
    view.classList.toggle(
      "active",
      view.dataset.view === name
    );
  });

  $$(".nav-btn").forEach(button => {
    button.classList.toggle(
      "active",
      button.dataset.target === name
    );
  });

  const index =
    VIEWS.indexOf(name);

  $("#progress").style.width =
    `${((index + 1) / VIEWS.length) * 100}%`;

  history.replaceState(
    null,
    "",
    "#" + name
  );

  const active =
    document.querySelector(
      `.view[data-view="${name}"]`
    );

  if (active) {
    active.scrollTop = 0;
    animateCounters(active);
  }
}

function openDrawer(
  type,
  title,
  value
) {

  $("#drawerType").textContent =
    type;

  $("#drawerTitle").textContent =
    title;

  $("#drawerMeta").textContent =
    "Verified snapshot projection";

  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML =
    `<pre>${esc(
      JSON.stringify(
        value,
        null,
        2
      )
    )}</pre>`;

  $("#drawer")
    .classList.add("open");
}

function closeDrawer() {
  $("#drawer")
    .classList.remove("open");
}

function renderMetrics(data) {

  const m =
    data.metrics || {};

  const max =
    Math.max(
      ...Object.values(m)
        .filter(Number.isFinite),
      1
    );

  const bar = value =>
    Math.max(
      7,
      Math.min(
        100,
        Math.log10(
          Number(value || 0) + 1
        ) /
        Math.log10(max + 1) *
        100
      )
    );

  $("#metrics").innerHTML = [
    metric(
      "Files",
      m.files,
      bar(m.files)
    ),
    metric(
      "Relationships",
      m.constitutional_relationships,
      bar(m.constitutional_relationships)
    ),
    metric(
      "Disciplines",
      m.kindred_disciplines,
      bar(m.kindred_disciplines)
    ),
    metric(
      "Tests discovered",
      m.test_functions,
      bar(m.test_functions)
    )
  ].join("");

  $("#runtimeMetrics").innerHTML = [
    metric(
      "Files",
      m.files,
      bar(m.files)
    ),
    metric(
      "Directories",
      m.directories,
      bar(m.directories)
    ),
    metric(
      "Authority records",
      m.authority_records,
      bar(m.authority_records)
    ),
    metric(
      "Exiles",
      m.exiles,
      bar(m.exiles)
    ),
    metric(
      "Relationships",
      m.constitutional_relationships,
      bar(m.constitutional_relationships)
    ),
    metric(
      "Disciplines",
      m.kindred_disciplines,
      bar(m.kindred_disciplines)
    ),
    metric(
      "Test files",
      m.test_files,
      bar(m.test_files)
    ),
    metric(
      "Test functions",
      m.test_functions,
      bar(m.test_functions)
    )
  ].join("");

  animateCounters();
}

function renderAuthority(data) {

  const statuses =
    data.authority_statuses || {};

  const total =
    Object.values(statuses)
      .reduce(
        (a,b) => a + Number(b || 0),
        0
      );

  $("#authoritySummary").textContent =
    `${fmt(total)} traced authority records`;

  $("#authorityPills").innerHTML =
    Object.entries(statuses)
      .map(([key,value]) => `
        <span class="pill">
          ${esc(key)} · ${fmt(value)}
        </span>
      `)
      .join("");
}

function renderedifice(data) {

  $("#edifice").innerHTML =
    (data.identity_edifice || [])
      .map((name,index) => `
        <div class="tier">

          <div class="tier-box">
            <div class="tier-name">
              ${esc(name)}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="tier-number">
            ${String(index + 1).padStart(2,"0")}
          </div>

        </div>
      `)
      .join("");
}

function createItem(
  index,
  title,
  copy,
  value,
  type,
  badge = ""
) {

  const element =
    document.createElement("article");

  element.className =
    "card item";

  element.innerHTML = `
    <div class="item-index">
      ${String(index + 1).padStart(2,"0")}
    </div>

    <h3>
      ${esc(title)}
    </h3>

    <p>
      ${esc(copy || "")}
    </p>

    ${
      badge
        ? `
          <span class="badge ${esc(badge)}">
            ${esc(badge)}
          </span>
        `
        : ""
    }
  `;

  element.addEventListener(
    "click",
    () => openDrawer(
      type,
      title,
      value
    )
  );

  return element;
}

function renderSubstrates(data) {

  const root =
    $("#substrates");

  root.innerHTML = "";

  (data.living_substrates || [])
    .forEach((item,index) => {

      const title =
        item.id ||
        item.name ||
        `Substrate ${index + 1}`;

      root.appendChild(
        createItem(
          index,
          titleCase(title),
          item.purpose || "",
          item,
          "Living Substrate"
        )
      );
    });
}

function renderDisciplines(data) {

  const root =
    $("#disciplines");

  root.innerHTML = "";

  (data.kindred_disciplines || [])
    .forEach((item,index) => {

      const title =
        item.canonical ||
        item.name ||
        item.id ||
        `Discipline ${index + 1}`;

      const relationCount =
        Array.isArray(item.relations)
          ? item.relations.length
          : 0;

      const copy =
        [
          relationCount
            ? `${relationCount} relationship forms`
            : "Relational discipline",
          item.steward || ""
        ]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(" · ");

      root.appendChild(
        createItem(
          index,
          titleCase(title),
          copy,
          item,
          "Kindred Discipline"
        )
      );
    });
}

function renderAuthorities(data) {

  const root =
    $("#authorities");

  root.innerHTML = "";

  (data.authority_records || [])
    .forEach((item,index) => {

      const title =
        item.title ||
        item.id ||
        item.path ||
        `Authority ${index + 1}`;

      const element =
        createItem(
          index,
          title,
          item.path || item.kind || "",
          item,
          "Authority Record",
          item.status || ""
        );

      root.appendChild(element);
    });
}

function renderAI(data) {

  const ai =
    data.ai || {};

  const mode =
    String(
      ai.mode || "native"
    ).toUpperCase();

  $("#aiMode").textContent =
    mode;

  $("#aiDescription").textContent =
    mode === "NATIVE"
      ? (
        "Savant is operating without external AI credentials. " +
        "Its native architecture remains valid and usable. " +
        "Configured providers can add capabilities without " +
        "changing the identity of the elements that consume them."
      )
      : (
        "External AI capability is active while Savant retains " +
        "native fallback. Provider activation augments execution " +
        "rather than becoming a new source of structural identity."
      );

  const providers =
    ai.providers || {};

  const root =
    $("#providers");

  if (!Object.keys(providers).length) {

    root.innerHTML = `
      <div class="provider">

        <div class="provider-dot"></div>

        <div class="provider-name">
          Provider registry
        </div>

        <div class="provider-state">
          native
        </div>

      </div>
    `;

    return;
  }

  root.innerHTML =
    Object.entries(providers)
      .map(([name,provider]) => `
        <div class="
          provider
          ${provider.usable ? "usable" : ""}
        ">

          <div class="provider-dot"></div>

          <div class="provider-name">
            ${esc(name)}
          </div>

          <div class="provider-state">
            ${
              provider.usable
                ? "active"
                : esc(
                    provider.reason ||
                    "inactive"
                  )
            }
          </div>

        </div>
      `)
      .join("");
}

function renderExiles(data) {

  const root =
    $("#exiles");

  root.innerHTML = "";

  (data.exiles || [])
    .forEach((item,index) => {

      const title =
        item.title ||
        item.name ||
        item.id ||
        `Exile ${index + 1}`;

      const purpose =
        Array.isArray(item.purpose)
          ? item.purpose.join(" · ")
          : item.purpose || "";

      root.appendChild(
        createItem(
          index,
          title,
          purpose,
          item,
          "Exile",
          item.status || ""
        )
      );
    });
}

function renderWhy(data) {

  const why =
    data.presentation?.why || [
      "To preserve meaning instead of merely storing output.",
      "To make complex systems composable instead of disposable.",
      "To keep authority, provenance and history visible.",
      "To let intelligence augment structure without owning it.",
      "To build something capable of becoming more than a collection of scripts."
    ];

  $("#whyList").innerHTML =
    why.map(line => `
      <div class="why-line">
        ${esc(line)}
      </div>
    `).join("");
}

function buildSearch(data) {

  const items = [];

  function add(
    type,
    title,
    value
  ) {

    if (!title) {
      return;
    }

    items.push({
      type,
      title: String(title),
      value
    });
  }

  (data.exiles || [])
    .forEach(item =>
      add(
        "Exile",
        item.title ||
        item.name ||
        item.id,
        item
      )
    );

  (data.authority_records || [])
    .forEach(item =>
      add(
        "Authority",
        item.title ||
        item.id ||
        item.path,
        item
      )
    );

  (data.kindred_disciplines || [])
    .forEach(item =>
      add(
        "Discipline",
        item.canonical ||
        item.name ||
        item.id,
        item
      )
    );

  (data.constitutional_relationships || [])
    .forEach(item =>
      add(
        "Relationship",
        item.name ||
        item.id ||
        item.canonical,
        item
      )
    );

  (data.living_substrates || [])
    .forEach(item =>
      add(
        "Substrate",
        item.id ||
        item.name,
        item
      )
    );

  state.search = items;
}

function showSearch() {

  $("#command")
    .classList.add("open");

  $("#commandInput").value =
    "";

  renderSearch("");

  setTimeout(
    () =>
      $("#commandInput").focus(),
    20
  );
}

function hideSearch() {
  $("#command")
    .classList.remove("open");
}

function renderSearch(query) {

  const q =
    query
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();

  const results =
    state.search
      .filter(item => {

        if (!q) {
          return true;
        }

        const corpus =
          `${item.title} ${item.type} ${
            JSON.stringify(item.value)
          }`
            .toLowerCase();

        return corpus.includes(q);
      })
      .slice(0,32);

  const root =
    $("#commandResults");

  root.innerHTML = "";

  results.forEach(item => {

    const row =
      document.createElement("div");

    row.className =
      "result";

    row.innerHTML = `
      <strong>
        ${esc(item.title)}
      </strong>

      <span>
        ${esc(item.type)}
      </span>
    `;

    row.addEventListener(
      "click",
      () => {
        hideSearch();

        openDrawer(
          item.type,
          item.title,
          item.value
        );
      }
    );

    root.appendChild(row);
  });
}

/* BACKGROUND FIELD */

function createSpace() {

  const canvas =
    $("#space");

  const ctx =
    canvas.getContext("2d");

  let width = 0;
  let height = 0;
  let stars = [];

  function resize() {

    const dpr =
      Math.min(
        window.devicePixelRatio || 1,
        2
      );

    width = innerWidth;
    height = innerHeight;

    canvas.width =
      width * dpr;

    canvas.height =
      height * dpr;

    canvas.style.width =
      width + "px";

    canvas.style.height =
      height + "px";

    ctx.setTransform(
      dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0
    );

    const count =
      Math.min(
        210,
        Math.max(
          70,
          Math.floor(
            width * height / 6500
          )
        )
      );

    stars =
      Array.from(
        {length: count},
        () => ({
          x: Math.random() * width,
          y: Math.random() * height,
          r: Math.random() * 1.15 + .15,
          a: Math.random() * .48 + .05,
          d: Math.random() * .15 + .02
        })
      );
  }

  let pointerX = 0;
  let pointerY = 0;

  addEventListener(
    "pointermove",
    event => {

      pointerX =
        event.clientX - width / 2;

      pointerY =
        event.clientY - height / 2;

      document.documentElement
        .style.setProperty(
          "--mx",
          `${event.clientX}px`
        );

      document.documentElement
        .style.setProperty(
          "--my",
          `${event.clientY}px`
        );
    },
    {passive: true}
  );

  function draw() {

    ctx.clearRect(
      0,0,width,height
    );

    for (const star of stars) {

      const x =
        star.x +
        pointerX * star.d * .025;

      const y =
        star.y +
        pointerY * star.d * .025;

      ctx.beginPath();

      ctx.arc(
        x,
        y,
        star.r,
        0,
        Math.PI * 2
      );

      ctx.fillStyle =
        `rgba(255,255,255,${star.a})`;

      ctx.fill();
    }

    requestAnimationFrame(draw);
  }

  resize();

  addEventListener(
    "resize",
    resize
  );

  draw();
}

/* INTERACTIVE TOPOLOGY */

function createGraph(data) {

  const canvas =
    $("#graph");

  const ctx =
    canvas.getContext("2d");

  let width = 0;
  let height = 0;
  let dpr = 1;

  const source =
    (data.exiles || [])
      .slice(0,18);

  const nodes =
    source.map(
      (item,index) => ({
        item,
        index,
        angle:
          Math.PI * 2 *
          index /
          Math.max(source.length,1),
        radius:
          .33 +
          (index % 3) * .055,
        x: 0,
        y: 0,
        vx: 0,
        vy: 0,
        hover: false
      })
    );

  let pointer = {
    x: -9999,
    y: -9999,
    down: false
  };

  function resize() {

    const rect =
      canvas.getBoundingClientRect();

    width = rect.width;
    height = rect.height;

    dpr =
      Math.min(
        window.devicePixelRatio || 1,
        2
      );

    canvas.width =
      width * dpr;

    canvas.height =
      height * dpr;

    ctx.setTransform(
      dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0
    );
  }

  function target(node,time) {

    const min =
      Math.min(width,height);

    const wobble =
      Math.sin(
        time * .00022 +
        node.index * 1.7
      ) * 7;

    return {
      x:
        width / 2 +
        Math.cos(
          node.angle +
          time * .000025 *
          (node.index % 2 ? 1 : -1)
        ) *
        min *
        node.radius +
        wobble,

      y:
        height / 2 +
        Math.sin(
          node.angle +
          time * .000025 *
          (node.index % 2 ? 1 : -1)
        ) *
        min *
        node.radius *
        .78
    };
  }

  function label(node) {

    return (
      node.item.title ||
      node.item.name ||
      node.item.id ||
      `Exile ${node.index + 1}`
    );
  }

  function draw(time) {

    ctx.clearRect(
      0,0,width,height
    );

    const cx =
      width / 2;

    const cy =
      height / 2;

    const min =
      Math.min(width,height);

    /* orbital fields */

    for (
      const factor of
      [.18,.31,.43]
    ) {

      ctx.beginPath();

      ctx.ellipse(
        cx,
        cy,
        min * factor,
        min * factor * .78,
        0,
        0,
        Math.PI * 2
      );

      ctx.strokeStyle =
        "rgba(255,255,255,.055)";

      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.stroke();
    }

    /* center */

    ctx.save();

    ctx.translate(cx,cy);

    ctx.rotate(
      Math.PI / 4 +
      time * .000035
    );

    ctx.strokeStyle =
      "rgba(217,181,109,.78)";

    ctx.lineWidth = 1;

    ctx.strokeRect(
      -44,
      -44,
      88,
      88
    );

    ctx.strokeStyle =
      "rgba(217,181,109,.25)";

    ctx.strokeRect(
      -27,
      -27,
      54,
      54
    );

    ctx.restore();

    /* update */

    nodes.forEach(node => {

      const t =
        target(node,time);

      node.vx +=
        (t.x - node.x) * .003;

      node.vy +=
        (t.y - node.y) * .003;

      node.vx *= .93;
      node.vy *= .93;

      node.x += node.vx;
      node.y += node.vy;

      if (
        !Number.isFinite(node.x) ||
        node.x === 0
      ) {
        node.x = t.x;
        node.y = t.y;
      }

      const dx =
        pointer.x - node.x;

      const dy =
        pointer.y - node.y;

      node.hover =
        Math.hypot(dx,dy) < 31;
    });

    /* graph edges */

    nodes.forEach((node,index) => {

      const next =
        nodes[
          (index + 1) %
          nodes.length
        ];

      if (!next) {
        return;
      }

      ctx.beginPath();

      ctx.moveTo(
        node.x,
        node.y
      );

      ctx.lineTo(
        next.x,
        next.y
      );

      ctx.strokeStyle =
        "rgba(217,181,109,.075)";

      ctx.stroke();

      ctx.beginPath();

      ctx.moveTo(
        cx,
        cy
      );

      ctx.lineTo(
        node.x,
        node.y
      );

      ctx.strokeStyle =
        node.hover
          ? "rgba(217,181,109,.34)"
          : "rgba(255,255,255,.045)";

      ctx.stroke();
    });

    /* nodes */

    nodes.forEach(node => {

      const active =
        node.hover;

      const r =
        active ? 25 : 21;

      ctx.beginPath();

      ctx.arc(
        node.x,
        node.y,
        r,
        0,
        Math.PI * 2
      );

      ctx.fillStyle =
        active
          ? "rgba(217,181,109,.12)"
          : "rgba(7,9,13,.88)";

      ctx.fill();

      ctx.strokeStyle =
        active
          ? "rgba(217,181,109,.85)"
          : "rgba(255,255,255,.16)";

      ctx.stroke();

      ctx.fillStyle =
        active
          ? "#f0d59b"
          : "#c5c7cc";

      ctx.font =
        "8px Inter, sans-serif";

      ctx.textAlign =
        "center";

      ctx.textBaseline =
        "middle";

      let text =
        label(node)
          .toUpperCase();

      if (text.length > 12) {
        text =
          text.slice(0,11) + "…";
      }

      ctx.fillText(
        text,
        node.x,
        node.y
      );
    });

    requestAnimationFrame(draw);
  }

  function point(event) {

    const rect =
      canvas.getBoundingClientRect();

    pointer.x =
      event.clientX - rect.left;

    pointer.y =
      event.clientY - rect.top;
  }

  canvas.addEventListener(
    "pointermove",
    event => {

      point(event);

      if (pointer.down) {

        nodes.forEach(node => {

          const dx =
            node.x - pointer.x;

          const dy =
            node.y - pointer.y;

          const distance =
            Math.max(
              30,
              Math.hypot(dx,dy)
            );

          if (distance < 120) {

            node.vx +=
              dx / distance * 1.8;

            node.vy +=
              dy / distance * 1.8;
          }
        });
      }

      canvas.style.cursor =
        nodes.some(
          node => node.hover
        )
          ? "pointer"
          : "crosshair";
    }
  );

  canvas.addEventListener(
    "pointerdown",
    event => {
      pointer.down = true;
      point(event);
    }
  );

  addEventListener(
    "pointerup",
    () => {
      pointer.down = false;
    }
  );

  canvas.addEventListener(
    "click",
    event => {

      point(event);

      const node =
        nodes.find(
          item =>
            Math.hypot(
              pointer.x - item.x,
              pointer.y - item.y
            ) < 32
        );

      if (node) {

        openDrawer(
          "Exile",
          label(node),
          node.item
        );
      }
    }
  );

  resize();

  addEventListener(
    "resize",
    resize
  );

  requestAnimationFrame(draw);

  state.graph = {
    nodes
  };
}

/* CARD LIGHTING */

function enableCardLighting() {

  document.addEventListener(
    "pointermove",
    event => {

      const card =
        event.target.closest(".card");

      if (!card) {
        return;
      }

      const rect =
        card.getBoundingClientRect();

      card.style.setProperty(
        "--card-x",
        `${event.clientX - rect.left}px`
      );

      card.style.setProperty(
        "--card-y",
        `${event.clientY - rect.top}px`
      );
    },
    {passive: true}
  );
}

/* TOUR */

const TOUR = [
  {
    view: "system",
    title: "Savant as a system",
    copy:
      "The Observatory begins with the measured runtime: topology, authority and scale."
  },
  {
    view: "architecture",
    title: "Recursive composition",
    copy:
      "Identity is organized hierarchically while reusable substrates describe behavior."
  },
  {
    view: "kindred",
    title: "Kindred",
    copy:
      "Relationships are promoted into reusable, graph-addressable infrastructure."
  },
  {
    view: "canon",
    title: "Canon",
    copy:
      "Authority remains traceable through status, source, lineage and provenance."
  },
  {
    view: "ai",
    title: "AI without dependency",
    copy:
      "AI capability augments Savant. The architecture remains valid without provider credentials."
  },
  {
    view: "runtime",
    title: "Measured implementation",
    copy:
      "The numbers shown here are generated from the runtime snapshot rather than typed into the presentation."
  },
  {
    view: "why",
    title: "Why it exists",
    copy:
      "Savant is an attempt to make complex creative and computational systems retain meaning, history and extensibility."
  }
];

function stopTour() {

  state.tour = false;

  clearTimeout(
    state.tourTimer
  );

  $("#tourBtn").textContent =
    "Tour";

  $("#tourToast")
    .classList.remove("show");
}

function startTour() {

  if (state.tour) {
    stopTour();
    return;
  }

  state.tour = true;

  $("#tourBtn").textContent =
    "Stop";

  let index = 0;

  function step() {

    if (!state.tour) {
      return;
    }

    const item =
      TOUR[index];

    navigate(item.view);

    $("#tourTitle").textContent =
      item.title;

    $("#tourCopy").textContent =
      item.copy;

    $("#tourToast")
      .classList.add("show");

    index++;

    if (index >= TOUR.length) {

      state.tourTimer =
        setTimeout(
          stopTour,
          7000
        );

      return;
    }

    state.tourTimer =
      setTimeout(
        step,
        7000
      );
  }

  step();
}

/* FULLSCREEN */

async function toggleFullscreen() {

  try {

    if (!document.fullscreenElement) {

      await document.documentElement
        .requestFullscreen();

      $("#fullBtn").textContent =
        "Exit";
    } else {

      await document
        .exitFullscreen();

      $("#fullBtn").textContent =
        "Fullscreen";
    }

  } catch (_) {
    /* browser may deny fullscreen */
  }
}

/* RENDER */

function render(data) {

  state.data =
    data;

  const metrics =
    data.metrics || {};

  $("#runtimeState").textContent =
    `Verified snapshot · ${
      fmt(metrics.files)
    } files`;

  $("#snapshotHash").textContent =
    "SHA · " +
    String(
      data.snapshot_sha256 || ""
    ).slice(0,16);

  renderMetrics(data);
  renderAuthority(data);
  renderedifice(data);
  renderSubstrates(data);
  renderDisciplines(data);
  renderAuthorities(data);
  renderAI(data);
  renderExiles(data);
  renderWhy(data);
  buildSearch(data);
  createGraph(data);

  const requested =
    location.hash
      .replace("#","");

  if (VIEWS.includes(requested)) {
    navigate(requested);
  } else {
    navigate("system");
  }
}

  .forEach(button => {

    button.addEventListener(
      "click",

        stopTour();

        navigate(
          button.dataset.target
        );
      }
    );
  });

      $("#boot")
        .classList.add("gone");

      );
    }
  );

    closeDrawer
  );

$("#searchBtn")

    showSearch
  );

$("#tourBtn")
  .addEventListener(
    "click",
    startTour
  );

$("#fullBtn")
  .addEventListener(

  );

$("#command")
  .addEventListener(
    "click",
    event => {

      if (
        event.target ===
        $("#command")
      ) {
        hideSearch();
      }
    }
  );

$("#commandInput")
  .addEventListener(
    "input",
    event =>
      renderSearch(
        event.target.value
      )
  );

document.addEventListener(
  "keydown",
  event => {

    const typing =
      ["INPUT","TEXTAREA"]
        .includes(
          document.activeElement.tagName
        );

    if (
      event.key === "/" &&
      !typing
    ) {

      event.preventDefault();
      showSearch();
      return;
    }

    if (
      (event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) &&
      event.key.toLowerCase() === "k"
    ) {

      event.preventDefault();
      showSearch();
      return;
    }

    if (event.key === "Escape") {

      hideSearch();
      closeDrawer();
      stopTour();

      return;
    }

    if (
      !typing &&
      event.key >= "1" &&
      event.key <= "7"
    ) {

      navigate(
        VIEWS[
          Number(event.key) - 1
        ]
      );
    }

    if (
      !typing &&
      event.key === "ArrowRight"
    ) {

      const index =
        VIEWS.indexOf(
          state.current
        );

      navigate(
        VIEWS[
          Math.min(
            VIEWS.length - 1,
            index + 1
          )
        ]
      );
    }

    if (
      !typing &&
      event.key === "ArrowLeft"
    ) {

      const index =
        VIEWS.indexOf(
          state.current
        );

      navigate(
        VIEWS[
          Math.max(
            0,
            index - 1
          )
        ]
      );
    }
  }
);

/* INIT */

async function init() {

  createSpace();
  enableCardLighting();

  try {

    const response =
      await fetch(
        "../data/savant.snapshot.json",
        {
          cache: "no-store"
        }
      );

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(
        `HTTP ${response.status}`
      );
    }

    const data =
      await response.json();

    render(data);

  } catch (error) {

    console.error(error);

    $("#error")
      .classList.add("show");
  }
}

init();
</script>

<script>
/* ENHANCEMENT CONTROLLER v2 */
(function(){
  const navButtons=[...document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn')];
  navButtons.forEach((b,i)=>b.dataset.index=String(i+1).padStart(2,'0'));
  const orbit=document.getElementById('cursorOrbit');
  document.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(orbit){orbit.style.left=e.clientX+'px';orbit.style.top=e.clientY+'px'};document.body.classList.toggle('pointer-hot',!!e.target.closest('button,.card,.result,[data-inspect]'))},{passive:true});
  let toastTimer; window.savantToast=function(msg){const t=document.getElementById('toast');if(!t)return;t.textContent=msg;t.classList.add('show');clearTimeout(toastTimer);toastTimer=setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('show'),1700)};
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(!document.hidden && state.data) savantToast('Observatory synchronized')});
  window.addEventListener('online',()=>savantToast('Network restored'));
  window.addEventListener('offline',()=>savantToast('Offline — current snapshot retained'));
  document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='?'&&!['INPUT','TEXTAREA'].includes(document.activeElement.tagName)){e.preventDefault();savantToast('Keys: 1–7 views · ← → navigate · / search · Esc close')}});
  let touchX=null; document.querySelector('.stage').addEventListener('touchstart',e=>{touchX=e.changedTouches[0].clientX},{passive:true});
  document.querySelector('.stage').addEventListener('touchend',e=>{if(touchX===null)return;const dx=e.changedTouches[0].clientX-touchX;touchX=null;if(Math.abs(dx)<70)return;const i=VIEWS.indexOf(state.current);navigate(VIEWS[Math.max(0,Math.min(VIEWS.length-1,i+(dx<0?1:-1)))])},{passive:true});
  const observer=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>entries.forEach(x=>{if(x.isIntersecting)x.target.classList.add('observed')}),{threshold:.12});
  document.querySelectorAll('.card').forEach(x=>observer.observe(x));
  document.addEventListener('dblclick',e=>{const card=e.target.closest('.card');if(card){card.animate([{transform:'scale(1)'},{transform:'scale(1.012)'},{transform:'scale(1)'}],{duration:320,easing:'cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1)'})}});
  document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange',()=>{const b=document.getElementById('fullBtn');if(b)b.textContent=document.fullscreenElement?'Exit':'Fullscreen'});
})();
</script>

<script>
/* FOUNDER'S DEMONSTRATION CONTROLLER v3 */
(function(){
  const $d=s=>document.querySelector(s);
  const hud=$d("#demoHud");
  if(!hud) return;

  let index=0,timer=null,paused=false;
  const duration=9000;

  function data(){return (window.state&&window.state.data)||null}
  function metric(key,fallback="—"){
    const d=data(); return d&&d.metrics&&d.metrics[key]!=null ? Number(d.metrics[key]).toLocaleString() : fallback;
  }
  function p(){const d=data();return d&&d.presentation?d.presentation:{}}
  function why(i,fallback){const a=p().why||[];return a[i]||fallback}

  const slides=[
    ()=>({view:"system",k:"01 · Evidence",t:"This is not a mock statistic.",c:`The Observatory is reading a verified runtime projection: ${metric("files")} files across ${metric("directories")} directories. The interface is disposable; the runtime remains the source.`,m:[`${metric("authority_records")} authority records`,`${metric("test_functions")} test functions`]}),
    ()=>({view:"architecture",k:"02 · Architecture",t:"Complexity without duplication.",c:"Savant separates recursive identity from reusable implementation substrate. Higher structures emerge by composition, attachment, specialization and projection rather than copying lower-level substance.",m:["9 identity tiers","9 living substrates"]}),
    ()=>({view:"kindred",k:"03 · Relationships",t:"Relationship becomes infrastructure.",c:"Kindred makes dependency, lineage, provenance, evidence, compatibility, composition and other relational disciplines graph-addressable and reusable instead of scattering them through ad-hoc code.",m:[`${metric("kindred_disciplines")} disciplines`,`${metric("constitutional_relationships")} constitutional relations`]}),
    ()=>({view:"canon",k:"04 · Memory",t:"The system remembers how it became true.",c:why(1,"Savant preserves history as it evolves.")+" "+why(2,"Authority, lineage and provenance remain visible."),m:["canonical ≠ accepted ≠ provisional","supersession preserved"]}),
    ()=>({view:"ai",k:"05 · Intelligence",t:"AI is capability, not authority.",c:why(3,"Savant remains useful without an external AI provider and can become more capable when providers are available."),m:[`mode: ${(data()?.ai?.mode||"native")}`,"provider state is explicit"]}),
    ()=>({view:"runtime",k:"06 · Specialization",t:"Many owners. One composable system.",c:`The runtime currently projects ${metric("exiles")} Exiles. Each owns a bounded semantic responsibility while sharing reusable constitutional and relational machinery.`,m:["ownership remains explicit","extensions do not require replacement"]}),
    ()=>({view:"why",k:"07 · Purpose",t:"Why build Savant?",c:why(0,"To build complex intelligent software while preserving where knowledge came from.")+" "+why(4,"To make an ambitious system composable instead of brittle."),m:["lineage","provenance","replay","extensibility"]}),
    ()=>({view:"why",k:"08 · Dedication",t:p().dedication||"For Mom.",c:"This demonstration is a projection of the work itself: a system designed to grow without erasing its history, and to make the structure behind that growth visible.",m:[p().edition||"Founder's Demonstration",p().date||"August 2026"],final:true})
  ];

  function goView(v){
    if(typeof window.navigate==="function") window.navigate(v);
    else{
      document.querySelectorAll(".view").forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle("active",x.dataset.view===v));
      document.querySelectorAll(".nav-btn").forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle("active",x.dataset.target===v));
    }
  }
  function render(){
    const s=slides[index]();
    goView(s.view);
    $d("#demoKicker").textContent=s.k;
    $d("#demoTitle").textContent=s.t;
    $d("#demoTitle").classList.toggle("demo-final",!!s.final);
    $d("#demoCopy").textContent=s.c;
    $d("#demoMeta").innerHTML=s.m.map(x=>`<span>${String(x)}</span>`).join("<span>·</span>");
    $d("#demoProgress").style.width=((index+1)/slides.length*100)+"%";
    $d("#demoPrev").disabled=index===0;
    $d("#demoNext").textContent=index===slides.length-1?"Finish":"Next";
    schedule();
  }
  function schedule(){
    clearTimeout(timer);
    if(paused) return;
    timer=setTimeout(()=>{if(index<slides.length-1){index++;render()}else stop()},duration);
  }
  function start(){
    if(!data()) return;
    if(typeof window.stopTour==="function") window.stopTour();
    index=0;paused=false;
    document.body.classList.add("demo-mode");
    hud.classList.add("show");
    $d("#demoPause").textContent="Pause";
    render();
  }
  function stop(){
    clearTimeout(timer);
    hud.classList.remove("show");
    document.body.classList.remove("demo-mode");
    paused=false;
  }
  function next(){if(index<slides.length-1){index++;render()}else stop()}
  function prev(){if(index>0){index--;render()}}
  function pause(){paused=!paused;$d("#demoPause").textContent=paused?"Resume":"Pause";schedule()}

  const tour=$d("#tourBtn");
  if(tour){
    const clone=tour.cloneNode(true);
    tour.parentNode.replaceChild(clone,tour);
    clone.textContent="Demonstrate";
    clone.addEventListener("click",start);
  }
  $d("#demoNext").addEventListener("click",next);
  $d("#demoPrev").addEventListener("click",prev);
  $d("#demoPause").addEventListener("click",pause);
  $d("#demoExit").addEventListener("click",stop);
  document.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{
    if(!hud.classList.contains("show")) return;
    if(e.key==="Escape"){e.preventDefault();stop()}
    else if(e.key==="ArrowRight"){e.preventDefault();next()}
    else if(e.key==="ArrowLeft"){e.preventDefault();prev()}
    else if(e.key===" "){e.preventDefault();pause()}
  });
  window.startDemonstration=start;
})();
</script>

</body>
</html>
